# 2. 1.,1 3 J.t.

体系哈密顿量为

$$
H = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left(\nabla_ {1} ^ {2} + \nabla_ {2} ^ {2}\right) - \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(\frac {2}{r _ {1}} + \frac {2}{r _ {2}} - \frac {1}{| \pmb {r} _ {1} - \pmb {r} _ {2} |}\right),
$$

选取试探泼函数为

$$
\psi_ {1} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}, \boldsymbol {r} _ {2}\right) \equiv \frac {Z ^ {3}}{\pi a ^ {3}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- Z \left(r _ {1} + r _ {2}\right) / a},
$$

其中 $Z$ 为可调参数，计算出能量期望佳，即

$$
\langle H \rangle = \left[ 2 Z ^ {2} - 4 Z (Z - 2) - (5 / 4) Z \right] E _ {1} = \left[ - 2 Z ^ {2} + (2 7 / 4) Z \right] E _ {1},
$$

其中 $E _ { 1 }$ 为氢原子基态能量.令。 $\partial \left. H \right. / \partial Z = 0$ ，求出当 $Z = 2 7 / 1 6$ 时， $\langle H \rangle$ 最小为

$$
\langle H \rangle_ {\min } = \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {3}{2}\right) ^ {6} E _ {1} = - 7 7. 5 \mathrm {e V}.
$$

由变分原理，氨原子基态能量 $\leqslant - 7 7 . 5 \mathrm { e } \dot { \mathrm { V } }$ (实验值为 $- 7 9 \ \mathrm { e V } ,$ ).

# 3.氢分子离子

体系哈密顿量为

$$
H = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \nabla^ {2} - \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(\frac {1}{r} + \frac {1}{r ^ {\prime}}\right),
$$

选取试探波函数为

$$
\psi = A \left[ \psi_ {0} (r) + \psi_ {0} \left(r ^ {\prime}\right) \right],
$$

其中，

$$
\psi_ {0} (\boldsymbol {r}) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {\pi a ^ {3}}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- r / a}.
$$

量子化学家称此为原子轨道函数线性组合法.

电子能量的期望值为

$$
\langle H \rangle = \left[ 1 + 2 \frac {D + X}{1 + I} \right] E _ {1},
$$

其中，

$$
I = \mathrm {e} ^ {- R / a} \left[ 1 + \left(\frac {R}{a}\right) + \frac {1}{3} \left(\frac {R}{a}\right) ^ {2} \right], D = \frac {a}{R} - \left(1 + \frac {a}{R}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 R / a},
$$

$$
X \equiv a \left\langle \psi_ {0} (r) \right| \frac {1}{r} \left| \psi_ {0} \left(r ^ {\prime}\right) \right\rangle .
$$

质子-质子排斥作用有关的势能

$$
V _ {p p} = \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{R} = - \frac {2 a}{R} E _ {1}.
$$

# 4. 1.命事

假设两个质于处于静止状态，哈密顿量是

$$
\hat {H} = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left(\nabla_ {1} ^ {2} + \nabla_ {2} ^ {2}\right) + \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(\frac {1}{r _ {1 2}} + \frac {1}{R} - \frac {1}{r _ {1}} - \frac {1}{r _ {1} ^ {\prime}} - \frac {1}{r _ {2}} - \frac {1}{r _ {2} ^ {\prime}}\right),
$$

其中 $r _ { 1 }$ 和 $r _ { 1 } ^ { \prime }$ 分别是电子 与每个质子的距离， $r _ { 2 }$ 和 $r _ { 2 } ^ { \prime }$ 分别是电子 与每个质于的距离;六个势能项分别描述了两个电子之间的排斥，两个质子之间的排斥，以及每个电子对每个质子的吸引力.

试探波函数可以选取对称化波函数和反对称波函数

$$
\psi_ {+} (\boldsymbol {r _ {1}}, \boldsymbol {r _ {2}}) = A _ {+} \left[ \psi_ {0} (r _ {1}) \psi_ {0} (r _ {2} ^ {\prime}) + \psi_ {0} (r _ {1} ^ {\prime}) \psi_ {0} (r _ {2}) \right],
$$

$$
\psi_ {-} \left(\boldsymbol {r _ {1}}, \boldsymbol {r _ {2}}\right) = A _ {-} \left[ \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1}\right) \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2} ^ {\prime}\right) - \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1} ^ {\prime}\right) \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2}\right) \right].
$$

把所有对能量的贡献加起来:动能，电子一质子势能，电子一电子势能，以及质子质子势能，得到

$$
\left\langle H \right\rangle_ {\pm} = 2 E _ {1} \left[ 1 - \frac {a}{R} + \frac {2 D - D _ {2} \pm (2 I X - X _ {2})}{1 \pm I ^ {2}} \right].
$$

态 $\psi _ { + }$ 妥求将两个电子置于自旋单态组态中，而 $\psi _ { - }$ 意味着将它们置于自旋三重态组态中.只有当两个电子处于羊态时才发生键合，即共价键.

*习题 8.1 利用高斯试探波函数(方程 (8.2)) 求出下列情况下所能得基态能量的最低上限: (8) 线性势 $V \left( x \right) = \alpha \left| x \right|$ j(b) 四次方势能 $V \left( x \right) = \alpha x ^ { 4 }$

解答取高斯型函数作为试探披函数

$$
\psi (x) = A \mathrm {e} ^ {- b x ^ {2}},
$$

其中 $b$ 为常数 $A$ 通过归一化条件可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} 1 = | A | ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x = | A | ^ {2} \sqrt {\frac {\pi}{2 b}} \\ \Rightarrow A = \left(\frac {2 b}{\pi}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{4}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以动能项为

$$
\langle T \rangle = - \frac {\hbar}{2 m} A ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- b x ^ {2}} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {- b x ^ {2}}\right) \mathrm {d} x = \frac {\hbar^ {2} b}{2 m}.
$$

(时线性势 $V \left( x \right) = \alpha \left| x \right|$ 的势能项

$$
\langle V \rangle = \alpha A ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} | x | \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x = 2 \alpha A ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} x \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x = 2 \alpha A ^ {2} \frac {1}{2 b} = \frac {\alpha}{\sqrt {2 b \pi}},
$$

$$
\langle H \rangle = \langle T \rangle + \langle V \rangle = \frac {\hbar^ {2} b}{2 m} + \frac {\alpha}{\sqrt {2 \pi b}}.
$$

对任意 $b , \langle H \rangle$ 必大于等于 $E _ { \mathrm { g s } }$ . 为了得到最佳上限，求 $\langle H \rangle$ 的最小值:

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} \left\langle H \right\rangle}{\mathrm {d} b} = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} - \frac {1}{2} \frac {\alpha}{\sqrt {2 \pi}} b ^ {- \frac {3}{2}} = 0 \Rightarrow b = \left(\frac {m \alpha}{\sqrt {2 \pi} \hbar^ {2}}\right) ^ {\frac {2}{3}},
$$

$$
\langle H \rangle_ {\mathrm {m i n}} = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left(\frac {m \alpha}{\sqrt {2 \pi} \hbar^ {2}}\right) ^ {\frac {2}{3}} + \frac {\alpha}{\sqrt {2 \pi}} \left(\frac {\sqrt {2 \pi} \hbar^ {2}}{m \alpha}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{3}} = \frac {3}{2} \left(\frac {\alpha^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 \pi m}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{3}}.
$$

(b) 四次方势能项

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle V \rangle = \alpha A ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} x ^ {4} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x = 2 \alpha A ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} x ^ {4} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x \\ = 2 \alpha A ^ {2} \frac {3}{8 (2 b) ^ {2}} \sqrt {\frac {\pi}{2 b}} = \frac {3 \alpha}{1 6 b ^ {2}}, \\ \langle H \rangle = \langle T \rangle + \langle V \rangle = \frac {\hbar^ {2} b}{2 m} + \frac {3 \alpha}{1 6 b ^ {2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

求 $\langle H \rangle$ 的最小值:

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} \left<   H \right > }{\mathrm {d} b} = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} - \frac {3 \alpha}{8 b ^ {3}} = 0 \Rightarrow b = \left(\frac {3 m \alpha}{4 \hbar^ {2}}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{3}},
$$

$$
\langle H \rangle_ {\mathrm {m i n}} = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left(\frac {m \alpha}{\sqrt {2 \pi} \hbar^ {2}}\right) ^ {\frac {2}{3}} + \frac {3 \alpha}{1 6} \left(\frac {4 \hbar^ {2}}{3 m \alpha}\right) ^ {\frac {2}{3}} = \frac {3}{4} \left(\frac {3 \alpha^ {2} \hbar^ {4}}{4 m ^ {2}}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{3}}.
$$

串串习题 8.2 取如下形式的试探被函数:

$$
\psi (x) = \frac {A}{x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}},
$$

求一维谐振子 $E _ { \mathrm { g s } }$ 的最佳上限，其中 由归一化确定 为可调参数.

解答 归一化披函数求 $A$

$$
1 = | A | ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \left(\frac {1}{x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x = 2 | A | ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \left(\frac {1}{x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x = 2 | A | ^ {2} \frac {\pi}{4 b ^ {3}} = \frac {\pi}{2 b ^ {3}} | A | ^ {2},
$$

$$
A = \sqrt {\frac {2 b ^ {3}}{\pi}}.
$$

动能项:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle T \rangle = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} | A | ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} \left(\frac {1}{x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}}\right) \mathrm {d} x \\ = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} | A | ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}} \frac {2 (3 x ^ {2} - b ^ {2})}{(x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}) ^ {3}} d x \\ = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} | A | ^ {2} 4 \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {3 (x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}) - 4 b ^ {2}}{(x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}) ^ {4}} \mathrm {d} x \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = - \frac {4 \hbar^ {2} b ^ {3}}{m \pi} \left[ \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {3}{\left(x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}\right) ^ {3}} d x - \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {4 b ^ {2}}{\left(x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}\right) ^ {4}} d x \right] \\ = - \frac {4 \hbar^ {2} b ^ {3}}{m \pi} \left(3 \frac {3 \pi}{1 6 b ^ {5}} - 4 b ^ {2} \frac {5 \pi}{3 2 b ^ {7}}\right) \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{4 m b ^ {2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

势能项:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle V \rangle = \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} | A | ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {x ^ {2}}{(x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}) ^ {2}} d x \\ = m \omega^ {2} | A | ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {x ^ {2} + b ^ {2} - b ^ {2}}{\left(x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}\right) ^ {2}} d x \\ = m \omega^ {2} | A | ^ {2} \left[ \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x - \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {b ^ {2}}{\left(x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}\right) ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x \right] \\ = m \omega^ {2} | A | ^ {2} \left(\frac {\pi}{2 b} - \frac {b ^ {2}}{2 b ^ {3}} \frac {\pi}{2}\right) \\ = m \omega^ {2} \frac {2 b ^ {3}}{\pi} \frac {\pi}{4 b} \\ = \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} b ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

则

$$
\langle H \rangle = \langle T \rangle + \langle V \rangle = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{4 m b ^ {2}} + \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} b ^ {2},
$$

$$
\frac {\partial \left<   H \right > }{\partial b} = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m b ^ {3}} + m \omega^ {2} b = 0 \Rightarrow b ^ {2} = \frac {\hbar}{\sqrt {2} m \omega},
$$

$$
\langle H \rangle_ {\mathrm {m i n}} = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{4 m} \frac {\sqrt {2} m \omega}{\hbar} + \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} \frac {\hbar}{\sqrt {2} m \omega} = \frac {\sqrt {2}}{2} \hbar \omega \geqslant \frac {1}{2} \hbar \omega .
$$

习题 8.3 取三角函数为试探波函数， $a$ 为可调参数. 求位于 $\delta$ 函数势 $V \left( x \right) =$ $- \alpha \delta \left( x \right)$ (方程 (8.10) ，中心在原点)中最佳上限 $E _ { \mathrm { g s } }$ .

解答 取试探披函数为

$$
\psi \left(x\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} {A \left(x + a / 2\right),} & {- a / 2 \leqslant x <   0,} \\ {A \left(a / 2 - x\right),} & {0 \leqslant x \leqslant a / 2,} \\ {0,} & {\text {其 他}.} \end{array} \right.
$$

归一化被函数可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} 1 = | A | ^ {2} \int_ {- a / 2} ^ {0} \left(x + \frac {a}{2}\right) ^ {2} d x + | A | ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {a / 2} \left(\frac {a}{2} - x\right) ^ {2} d x \\ = 2 \left| A \right| ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {a / 2} \left(\frac {a}{2} - x\right) ^ {2} d x \\ = \frac {a ^ {3}}{1 2} | A | ^ {2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
A = \sqrt {\frac {1 2}{a ^ {3}}}.
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} \psi}{\mathrm {d} x} = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} {A,} & {- a / 2 \leqslant x <   0,} \\ {- A,} & {0 \leqslant x \leqslant a / 2,} \\ {0,} & {\text {其 他}.} \end{array} \right.
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} \psi}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} = A \delta \left(x + \frac {a}{2}\right) - 2 A \delta (x) + A \delta \left(x - \frac {a}{2}\right).
$$

功能项:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle T \rangle = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \int \psi \left[ A \delta \left(x + \frac {a}{2}\right) - 2 A \delta (x) + A \delta \left(x - \frac {a}{2}\right) \right] d x \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} 2 A \psi (0) \\ = 6 \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{m a ^ {2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

势能项:

$$
\langle V \rangle = - \alpha \int | \psi | ^ {2} \delta (x) d x = - \alpha | \psi (0) | ^ {2} = - 3 \frac {\alpha}{a}.
$$

则

$$
\langle H \rangle = \langle T \rangle + \langle V \rangle = 6 \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{m a ^ {2}} - 3 \frac {\alpha}{a},
$$

$$
\frac {\partial \left<   H \right > }{\partial a} = - 1 2 \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{m a ^ {3}} + 3 \frac {\alpha}{a ^ {2}} = 0,
$$

$$
a = 4 \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{m \alpha},
$$

$$
\langle H \rangle_ {\min } = 6 \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{m} \left(\frac {m \alpha}{4 \hbar^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} - 3 \alpha \left(\frac {m \alpha}{4 \hbar^ {2}}\right) = - \frac {3 m \alpha^ {2}}{8 \hbar^ {2}} \geqslant - \frac {m \alpha^ {2}}{2 \hbar^ {2}}.
$$

(这是 势束缚态能量.)

# 习题 8.4

(a) 证明变分原理有如下推论:如果 $\langle \psi | \ \psi _ { \mathrm { g s } } \rangle = 0$ ，则 $\langle H \rangle \geqslant E _ { \mathrm { f e } }$ ，其中 $E _ { \mathrm { f e } }$ 是第一激发态的能量.注释如果可以找到一个试探波函数和严格的基态正交的话，就可以得到第一激发态的能量上限.一般来说，很难保证 $\psi$ 与 $\psi _ { \mathrm { g s } }$ 是正交的，因为(假定地)我们并不知道后者.然而，如果势能 $V \left( x \right)$ 是 $x$ 的偶函数，则基态也是偶函数因此任意奇的试探波函数将自动满足推论的条件.①

(b) 使用以下函数为试探波函数:

$$
\psi (x) = A x \mathrm {e} ^ {- b x ^ {2}},
$$

求一维谐振子的第一激发态能量最佳上限.

解答 (时任意披函数可以用能量本征函数展开为 $\psi = \sum _ { n = 1 } ^ { \infty } c _ { n } \psi _ { n }$ 其中 $\psi _ { 1 } = \psi _ { \mathrm { g s } }$ 因为基态披函数.

由于 $\langle \psi / \psi _ { \mathrm { g s } } \rangle = 0$ ，则

$$
\sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} c _ {n} \left\langle \psi_ {1} / \psi \right\rangle = c _ {1} = 0,
$$

基态的展开系数为 O. 所以

$$
\langle H \rangle = \sum_ {n = 2} ^ {\infty} E _ {n} \left| c _ {n} \right| ^ {2} \geqslant E _ {\mathrm {f e}} \sum_ {n = 2} ^ {\infty} \left| c _ {n} \right| ^ {2} = E _ {\mathrm {f e}}.
$$

因此 $\langle H \rangle > E _ { \mathrm { f e } }$ ，其中 $E _ { \mathrm { f e } }$ 是第一激发态的能量.

(b) 归一化试探披函数

$$
\begin{array}{l} 1 = | A | ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} x ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- b x ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x \\ = | A | ^ {2} 2 \frac {1}{8 b} \sqrt {\frac {\pi}{2 b}}, \\ \left| A \right| ^ {2} = 4 b \sqrt {\frac {2 b}{\pi}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

动能项:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle T \rangle = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} | A | ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} x \mathrm {e} ^ {- b x ^ {2}} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} \left(x \mathrm {e} ^ {- b x ^ {2}}\right) \mathrm {d} x \\ = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} | A | ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} x \mathrm {e} ^ {- b x ^ {2}} \left(- 6 b x \mathrm {e} ^ {- b x ^ {2}} + 4 b ^ {2} x ^ {3} \mathrm {e} ^ {- b x ^ {2}}\right) \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} | A | ^ {2} 6 b \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} x ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} | A | ^ {2} 4 b ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} x ^ {4} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} 4 b \sqrt {\frac {2 b}{\pi}} 2 \left(6 b \frac {1}{8 b} \sqrt {\frac {\pi}{2 b}} + 4 b ^ {2} \frac {3}{3 2 b ^ {2}} \sqrt {\frac {\pi}{2 b}}\right) \\ = \frac {3 b \hbar^ {2}}{2 m}. \\ \end{array}
$$

势能项:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle V \rangle = \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} | A | ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} x ^ {4} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} | A | ^ {2} 2 \frac {3}{3 2 b ^ {2}} \sqrt {\frac {\pi}{2 b}} = \frac {3 m \omega^ {2}}{8 b}. \\ \end{array}
$$

则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle H \rangle = \frac {3 b \hbar^ {2}}{2 m} + \frac {3 m \omega}{8 b}, \\ \frac {\partial \langle H \rangle}{\partial b} = \frac {3 \hbar^ {2}}{2 m} - \frac {3 m \omega^ {2}}{8 b ^ {2}} = 0, \\ b = \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar}, \\ \langle H \rangle_ {\mathrm {m i n}} = \frac {3 \hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} + \frac {3 m \omega^ {2}}{8} \frac {2 \hbar}{m \omega} = \frac {3}{2} \hbar \omega \geqslant \frac {3}{2} \hbar \omega . \\ \end{array}
$$

这是第一激发态能量的准确值，因为试探波函数是真实波函数的形式.

习题 8.5 使用自己设计的试探波函数，求"弹跳球"势能的基态能量上限(方程(2.185)) ，并将其与精确结果作对比(习题 259):EP =233811(mtf/2)1/3. $E _ { \mathrm { g s } } = 2 . 3 3 8 1 1 \left( m g ^ { 2 } \hbar ^ { 2 } / 2 \right) ^ { 1 / 3 }$

解答弹跳球势的形式是

$$
V \left(x\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} m g x, & x > 0, \\ \infty , & x \leqslant 0. \end{array} \right.
$$

可知试探波函数在 $x = 0$ 处为 ，且在 $x = \infty$ 处也为 O. 为保证波函数在 到 $\infty$ 围内单值、连续，试探波函数可采用如下形式: $\psi \left( x \right) = A x \mathrm { e } ^ { - b x }$ 或者 $\psi \left( x \right) = A x \mathrm { e } ^ { - b x ^ { 2 } }$ '其中 $b$ 为变分参数 $A$ 为归一化常数.

对于形式 $\psi \left( x \right) = A x \mathrm { e } ^ { - b x }$ 的波函数，先归一化求 $A$

$$
\begin{array}{l} 1 = \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} A ^ {2} x ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \mathrm {d} x \\ = A ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} x ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \mathrm {d} x \\ = A ^ {2} \left(- \frac {1}{2 b} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} x ^ {2} \mathrm {d e} ^ {- 2 b x}\right) \\ = A ^ {2} \left[ - \frac {1}{2 b} \left(x ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \Big | _ {0} ^ {\infty} - \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} 2 x \mathrm {d} x\right) \right] \\ = \frac {A ^ {2}}{b} \left(- \frac {1}{2 b} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} x \mathrm {d e} ^ {- 2 b x}\right) \\ = \frac {A ^ {2}}{b} \left(- \frac {1}{2 b}\right) \left(x \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \Big | _ {0} ^ {\infty} - \int \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \mathrm {d} x\right) \\ = - \frac {A ^ {2}}{2 b ^ {2}} \frac {1}{2 b} \int \mathrm {d e} ^ {- 2 b x} \\ = \frac {A ^ {2}}{4 b ^ {3}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
A = 2 b ^ {\frac {3}{2}}.
$$

由 ${ \frac { \operatorname { d } } { \operatorname { d } x } } = A { \frac { \operatorname { d } } { \operatorname { d } x } } \left( x \operatorname { e } ^ { - b x } \right) = A \left( 1 - b x \right) \operatorname { e } ^ { - b x }$ 动能部分为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle T \rangle = \langle \psi | \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} | \psi \rangle \\ = \frac {1}{2 m} \langle p \psi | p \psi \rangle \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left\langle \frac {\mathrm {d} \psi}{\mathrm {d} x} \right| \frac {\mathrm {d} \psi}{\mathrm {d} x} \Bigg \rangle \\ = \frac {A ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} (1 - b x) ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {A ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} (1 - 2 b x + b ^ {2} x ^ {2}) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {A ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left(\int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \mathrm {d} x - 2 b \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} x \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \mathrm {d} x + b ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} x ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \mathrm {d} x\right) \\ = \frac {A ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left(\left. \frac {1}{- 2 b} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \right| _ {0} ^ {\infty} + \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} x \mathrm {d e} ^ {- 2 b x} - \frac {b}{2} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} x ^ {2} \mathrm {d e} ^ {- 2 b x}\right) \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \frac {A ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left[ \left(0 + \frac {1}{2 b}\right) + \left(x \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \bigg | _ {0} ^ {\infty} - \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \mathrm {d} x\right) \right. \\ \left. - \frac {b}{2} \left(x ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \Big | _ {0} ^ {\infty} - \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} 2 x \mathrm {d} x\right) \right] \\ = \frac {A ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left(\frac {1}{2 b} + 0 - \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \mathrm {d} x + b \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} x \mathrm {d} x\right) \\ = \frac {A ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left[ \frac {1}{2 b} + \frac {1}{2 b} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \right| _ {0} ^ {\infty} + b \left(- \frac {1}{2 b}\right) \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} x \mathrm {d e} ^ {- 2 b x} \\ = \frac {A ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left[ \frac {1}{2 b} + 0 - \frac {1}{2 b} - \frac {1}{2} \left(x \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \bigg | _ {0} ^ {\infty} - \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \mathrm {d} x\right) \right] \\ = - \frac {A ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{4 m} \left(\frac {1}{2 b} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \Big | _ {0} ^ {\infty}\right) = \frac {A ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{8 m b} \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2} b ^ {2}}{2 m}. \\ \end{array}
$$

势能部分为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle V (x) \rangle = \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \psi^ {*} (x) V (x) \psi (x) d x = A ^ {2} m g \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} x ^ {3} e ^ {- 2 b x} d x \\ = A ^ {2} m g \left(- \frac {1}{2 b}\right) \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} x ^ {3} \mathrm {d e} ^ {- 2 b x} \\ = A ^ {2} m g \left(- \frac {1}{2 b}\right) \left(x ^ {3} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \Big | _ {0} ^ {\infty} - \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} 3 x ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x\right) \\ = A ^ {2} m g \frac {3}{2 b} \left(- \frac {1}{2 b}\right) \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} 3 x ^ {2} \mathrm {d e} ^ {- 2 b x} \\ = A ^ {2} m g \frac {- 3}{4 b ^ {2}} \left(3 x ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \Big | _ {0} ^ {\infty} - \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} 6 x \mathrm {d} x\right) \\ = A ^ {2} m g \frac {1 8}{4 b ^ {2}} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} x \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \mathrm {d} x = A ^ {2} m g \frac {- 1 8}{8 b ^ {3}} \left(x \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \Big | _ {0} ^ {\infty} - \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \mathrm {d} x\right) \\ = A ^ {2} m g \frac {1 8}{8 b ^ {3}} \left(- \frac {1}{2 b}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x} \Bigg | _ {0} ^ {\infty} \\ = \frac {9 m g}{2 b}. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此，能量的期望值为 $E \left( b \right) = \frac { \hbar ^ { 2 } b ^ { 2 } } { 2 m } + \frac { 9 m g } { 2 b }$

求基态能量的上限，

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} E (b)}{\mathrm {d} b} = \frac {\hbar^ {2} b}{m} - \frac {9 m g}{2 b ^ {2}} = 0,
$$

得 $b = \left( \frac { 9 m ^ { 2 } g } { 2 \hbar ^ { 2 } } \right) ^ { \frac { 1 } { 3 } }$ 代入 $E \left( b \right)$ ~

$$
\begin{array}{l} E (b) = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left(\frac {9 m ^ {2} g}{2 \hbar^ {2}}\right) ^ {\frac {2}{3}} + \frac {9 m g}{2} \left(\frac {2 \hbar^ {2}}{9 m ^ {2} g}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{3}} \\ = \left(\frac {9 ^ {\frac {2}{3}}}{2 ^ {\frac {4}{3}}} \frac {\hbar}{m} + 2\right) \left(\frac {m g ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{3}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

由于 $\hbar$ 为 $1 0 ^ { - 3 4 }$ 量级，所以 $E \left( b \right) \approx 2 \left( \frac { m g ^ { 2 } \hbar ^ { 2 } } { 2 } \right) ^ { \frac { 1 } { 3 } } < 2 . 3 3 8 1 1 \left( \frac { m g ^ { 2 } \hbar ^ { 2 } } { 2 } \right) ^ { \frac { 1 } { 3 } } = E _ { \mathrm { g s } } .$ 9%2飞

对于 $\psi \left( x \right) = A x \mathrm { e } ^ { - b x ^ { 2 } }$ ，归一化求 $A$

$$
\begin{array}{l} 1 = \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} | \psi (x) | ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x \\ = A ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} x ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x \\ = A ^ {2} \frac {1}{\sqrt {8 b ^ {3}}} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} u ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- u ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} u \\ = A ^ {2} \frac {1}{\sqrt {8 b ^ {3}}} \frac {\sqrt {\pi}}{4}, \\ \end{array}
$$

得

$$
A ^ {2} = 8 \sqrt {\frac {2 b ^ {3}}{\pi}}.
$$

由 ${ \frac { \mathrm { d } \psi \left( x \right) } { \mathrm { d } x } } = A \mathrm { e } ^ { - b x ^ { 2 } } - 2 b A x ^ { 2 } \mathrm { e } ^ { - b x ^ { 2 } }$ Afh22-2b ，动能部分为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle T \rangle = \langle \psi | \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} | \psi \rangle \\ = \frac {1}{2 m} \langle p \psi | p \psi \rangle \\ = \frac {A ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \left(1 - 2 b x ^ {2}\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} 8 \sqrt {\frac {2 b ^ {3}}{\pi}} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \lambda}} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} (1 - u ^ {2}) ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- u ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} u \\ = \frac {3 b \hbar^ {2}}{2 m}. \\ \end{array}
$$

势能部分为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle V \rangle = \langle \psi | V (x) | \psi \rangle \\ = A ^ {2} m g \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} x ^ {3} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 b x ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x \\ = 8 \sqrt {\frac {2 b ^ {3}}{\pi}} m g \frac {1}{(2 b) ^ {2}} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} u ^ {3} \mathrm {e} ^ {- u ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} u \\ = \frac {2}{\sqrt {\pi b}} m g. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此，能量的期望值为

$$
E (b) = \frac {3 b \hbar^ {2}}{2 m} + \frac {2}{\sqrt {\pi} b} m g.
$$

求基态能量上限

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathrm {d} E (b)}{\mathrm {d} b} = \frac {3 \hbar^ {2}}{2 m} + m g \sqrt {\frac {2}{\pi}} \left(- \frac {1}{2}\right) \frac {1}{b ^ {\frac {3}{2}}} = 0, \\ b = \left(\frac {2 m ^ {4} g ^ {2}}{9 \pi \hbar^ {4}}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{3}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

代入 $E \left( b \right)$ 中，得

$$
E \left(b\right) \leqslant 3 \left(\frac {3}{2 \pi}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{3}} \left(\frac {m g ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{3}} \approx 2. 3 4 5 \left(\frac {m g ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{3}}.
$$

# 习题 8.6

(a) 利用变分定理，证明:一阶非简并微扰理论总是高估(或者说在任何情况下从未低估过)基态能量.  
(b) 根据 (a) 的结论，你会期望基态的二阶修正总是负的.通过验证方程 (7.15)确认情况确实如此.

解答 (a) 对于一阶非简并微扰，不妨选取无微扰情况下的基态波函数 $\psi _ { \mathrm { g s } } ^ { 0 }$ 作为试探披函数.由变分原理

$$
E _ {\mathrm {g s}} \leqslant \langle \psi_ {0} | H | \psi_ {0} \rangle ,
$$

其中 $H = H ^ { 0 } + H ^ { \prime } , H _ { 0 }$ 为无微扰情况下的哈密顿量， $H ^ { \prime }$ 为微扰哈密顿量.代入上式，得

$$
E _ {\mathrm {g s}} \leqslant \left\langle \psi_ {0} \right| H _ {0} + H ^ {\prime} | \psi_ {0} \rangle = \left\langle \psi_ {0} \right| H _ {0} | \psi_ {0} \rangle + \left\langle \psi_ {0} \right| H ^ {\prime} | \psi_ {0} \rangle ,
$$

第一项 $E _ { \mathrm { g s } } ^ { 0 }$ 是无微扰情况下的基态能量，第二项为 $E _ { \mathrm { g s } } ^ { 1 }$ 基态能量的一阶修正.因此，

$$
E _ {\mathrm {g s}} \leqslant E _ {\mathrm {g s}} ^ {0} + E _ {\mathrm {g s}} ^ {1},
$$

所以一阶非筒并微扰理论永远高估了基态能量.

(b) 基态能量的二阶修正项为

$$
E _ {\mathrm {g s}} ^ {2} = \sum_ {\mathrm {g s} \neq m} \frac {\left| \left\langle \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right| H ^ {\prime} \left| \psi_ {\mathrm {g s}} ^ {0} \right\rangle \right| ^ {2}}{E _ {\mathrm {g s}} ^ {0} - E _ {m} ^ {0}}.
$$

等号右边分子显然为正，对于分母，由于所有的 $E _ { m } ^ { 0 } > E _ { \mathrm { g s } } ^ { 0 }$ 即分母恒负，所以 $E _ { \mathrm { g s } } ^ { 2 }$ 恒为负值.

习题 8.7 氯原子的基态能量取为 $E _ { \mathrm { g s } } = - 7 9 . 0 ~ \mathrm { e V } .$ ，计算电离能(移走一个电子所需要的能量).

提示 先计算只有一个电子绕原子核运动的氯离子 $\mathrm { H e } ^ { + }$ +的基态能量，然后两个能量相减.

解答根据能量守恒定律，计算出 $\mathrm { H e ^ { + } }$ +的基态能量，与氧的基态能量之差即为所求. $\mathrm { H e ^ { + } }$ +属于类氢原子 $( Z = 2$ ，其基态能量为

$$
E _ {1} = 2 ^ {2} \times (- 1 3. 6 \mathrm {e V}) = - 5 4. 4 \mathrm {e V}.
$$

所以，氮的电离能为

$$
7 9. 0 \mathrm {e V} - 5 4. 4 \mathrm {e V} = 2 4. 6 \mathrm {e V}.
$$

*习题 8.8 将本节的方法应用于 $\mathrm { H } ^ { - }$ 和 $\mathrm { L i ^ { + } }$ (与氨原子类似，它们都包含两个电子，但核电荷分别是 $Z = 1$ 和 $Z = 3$ ). 求出有效(部分屏蔽)核电荷，并确定每种情况$E _ { \mathrm { g s } }$ 的最佳上限，注释在 $\mathrm { H } ^ { - }$ 的情况中，你会发现 $\langle H \rangle > - 1 3 . 6 \ \mathrm { e V } .$ ，这表明根本不存在束缚态.因为从能量上看，这利于一个电子脱离原子核的束缚，留下中性的氢原子，这并不令人惊讶，因为电子受 $\mathrm { H } ^ { - }$ 原子核的吸引要比在氨核中小得多，而且

电子排斥倾向于使原子分裂.然而，事实证明这是不正确的.用更复杂的试探波函数(见习题 8.25) 可以证明 $\mathrm { E _ { g s } < - 1 3 . 6 ~ e V }$ ，因此确实存在束缚态.不过，它几乎没有束缚态，也没有激发态，①所以 $\mathrm { H } ^ { - }$ 没有离散的光谱(所有的跃迁都是从连续谱到连续谱).因此，尽管它在太阳表面大量存在，但很难在实验室进行研究.②

解答和氧原子类似，这里唯一不同的是 $Z$ 值的大小，其值分别为 所以，我们先求出任意$Z$ 值的情况.设核子有 $Z _ { 0 }$ 个质子，忽略两电于间相互排斥力，基态波函数为

$$
\psi_ {0} = \frac {Z _ {0} ^ {3}}{\pi a ^ {3}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- Z _ {0} (r _ {1} + r _ {2}) / a}.
$$

对应能量为 $2 Z _ { 0 } ^ { 2 } E _ { 1 }$ 电子间的排斥能为(对比方程 (8.21) $\sim$ (8.32) )

$$
\langle V _ {e e} \rangle = - \frac {5}{4} Z _ {0} E _ {1}.
$$

所以，

$$
\langle H \rangle = \left(2 Z _ {0} ^ {2} - \frac {5}{4} Z _ {0}\right) E _ {1}.
$$

考虑两个电子之间的相互作用使实际上的 $Z$ 要比 $Z _ { 0 }$ 小.故选取试探波函数

$$
\psi_ {1} \left(r _ {1}, r _ {2}\right) = \frac {Z ^ {3}}{\pi a ^ {3}} e ^ {- Z \left(r _ {1} + r _ {2}\right) / a}.
$$

计算 $\langle H \rangle$ 与教材中计算氨基态能量的方程 (8.29) $\mathrm { \ : Z _ { 0 } = 2 \ : }$ 对比，只需把对应公式中的 $Z - 2$ 换作$Z - Z _ { 0 }$ ，因此

$$
\langle H \rangle = \left[ 2 Z ^ {2} - 4 Z (Z - Z _ {0}) - \frac {5}{4} Z \right] E _ {1} = \left(- 2 Z ^ {2} + 4 Z Z _ {0} - \frac {5}{4} Z\right) E _ {1}.
$$

对 $Z$ 求微分求能量最小值，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\partial \langle H \rangle}{\partial Z} = \left(- 4 Z + 4 Z _ {0} - \frac {5}{4}\right) E _ {1} = 0 \\ \Rightarrow Z = Z _ {0} - \frac {5}{1 6}, \\ \end{array}
$$

所以，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle H \rangle_ {\mathrm {m i n}} = \left[ - 2 \left(Z _ {0} - \frac {5}{1 6}\right) ^ {2} + 4 \left(Z _ {0} - \frac {5}{1 6}\right) Z _ {0} - \frac {5}{4} \left(Z _ {0} - \frac {5}{1 6}\right) \right] E _ {1} \\ = \left(2 Z _ {0} ^ {2} - \frac {5}{4} Z _ {0} + \frac {2 5}{1 2 8}\right) E _ {1} = \frac {(1 6 Z _ {0} - 5) ^ {2}}{1 2 8} E _ {1}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} Z _ {0} = 1 \left(\mathrm {H} ^ {-}\right): Z = \frac {1 1}{1 6}, \langle H \rangle_ {\min } = \frac {1 2 1}{1 2 8} E _ {1} = - 1 2. 9 \mathrm {e V}, \\ Z _ {0} = 2 (\mathrm {H e}): Z = \frac {2 7}{1 6}, \langle H \rangle_ {\min } = \frac {6 3 9}{1 2 8} E _ {1} = - 7 7. 5 \mathrm {e V}, \\ Z _ {0} = 3 \left(\mathrm {L i} ^ {+}\right): Z = \frac {4 3}{1 6}, \langle H \rangle_ {\min } = \frac {1 8 4 9}{1 2 8} E _ {1} = - 1 9 6 \mathrm {e V}. \\ \end{array}
$$

事习题 8.9 计算 $D$ 和 $X$ (方程 (8.46) (8.47)). 用方程 (8 .48) (8 .49) 验证你的结果.

解答由方程 (8 .46)

$$
\begin{array}{l} D = a \left\langle \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1}\right) \right| \frac {1}{r _ {2}} \left| \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1}\right) \right\rangle = a \left\langle \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2}\right) \right| \frac {1}{r _ {1}} \left| \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2}\right) \right\rangle \\ = a \frac {1}{\pi a ^ {3}} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 r _ {2} / a} \frac {1}{r _ {1}} r ^ {2} \sin \theta \mathrm {d} r \mathrm {d} \theta \mathrm {d} \phi , \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $r _ { 1 }$ 是 $\mathrm { H _ { 2 } ^ { + } }$ 中电子到第一个核子的距离， $r _ { 2 }$ 是该电子到第二个核子的距离.与教材中计算 $I$ 时一样，我们设第一个核子处于坐标原点，所以

$$
r _ {1} = r, \quad r _ {2} = \sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2} - 2 r R \cos \theta}.
$$

故

$$
\begin{array}{l} D = a \frac {1}{\pi a ^ {3}} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {2}{a} \sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2} - 2 r R \cos \theta}} r \sin \theta \mathrm {d} r \mathrm {d} \theta \mathrm {d} \phi \\ = \frac {2 \pi}{\pi a ^ {2}} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} r \mathrm {d} r \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {2}{a} \sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2} - 2 r R \cos \theta}} \sin \theta \mathrm {d} \theta . \\ \end{array}
$$

令

$$
r ^ {2} + R ^ {2} - 2 r R \cos \theta = y ^ {2} \Rightarrow r R \sin \theta \mathrm {d} \theta = y \mathrm {d} y,
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {2}{a} \sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2} - 2 r R \cos \theta}} \sin \theta \mathrm {d} \theta \\ = \frac {1}{r R} \int_ {| R - r |} ^ {R + r} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 y / a} y \mathrm {d} y = \frac {1}{r R} \left. \left(- \frac {a}{2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 y / a} y - \frac {a ^ {2}}{4} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 y / a}\right) \right| _ {| R - r |} ^ {R + r} \\ = - \frac {a}{2 r R} \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 (R + r) / a} \left(R + r + \frac {a}{2}\right) - \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 | R - r | / a} \left(| R - r | + \frac {a}{2}\right) \right], \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} D = - \frac {1}{a R} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {d} r \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 (R + r) / a} \left(R + r + \frac {a}{2}\right) - \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 | R - r | / a} \left(| R - r | + \frac {a}{2}\right) \right] \\ = - \frac {1}{a R} \left[ \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {d} r \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 (R + r) / a} \left(R + r + \frac {a}{2}\right) - \int_ {0} ^ {R} \mathrm {d} r \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 (R - r) / a} \left(R - r + \frac {a}{2}\right) \right. \\ \left. - \int_ {R} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {d} r \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 (r - R) / a} \left(r - R + \frac {a}{2}\right) \right] \\ = - \frac {1}{a R} \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 R / a} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {d} r \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 r / a} \left(R + r + \frac {a}{2}\right) - \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 R / a} \int_ {0} ^ {R} \mathrm {d} r \mathrm {e} ^ {2 r / a} \left(R - r + \frac {a}{2}\right) \right. \\ \left. - \mathrm {e} ^ {2 R / a} \int_ {R} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {d} r \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 r / a} \left(r - R - \frac {a}{2}\right) \right] \\ = - \frac {1}{a R} \left\{\mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 R / a} \left[ R \frac {a}{2} + \left(\frac {a}{2}\right) ^ {2} + \left(\frac {a}{2}\right) ^ {2} \right] \right. \\ - \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 R / a} \left[ \frac {a}{2} \mathrm {e} ^ {2 r / a} \left(R - r + \frac {a}{2}\right) + \left(\frac {a}{2}\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {2 r / a} \right] \Bigg | _ {0} ^ {R} \\ - \mathrm {e} ^ {2 R / a} \left[ - \frac {a}{2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 r / a} \left(r - R + \frac {a}{2}\right) - \left(\frac {a}{2}\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 r / a} \right] \Bigg | _ {R} ^ {\infty} \Bigg \} \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = - \frac {1}{a R} \left\{\mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 R / a} \left[ R \frac {a}{2} + 2 \left(\frac {a}{2}\right) ^ {2} \right] - 2 \left(\frac {a}{2}\right) ^ {2} + \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 R / a} \frac {a}{2} (R + a) - 2 \left(\frac {a}{2}\right) ^ {2} \right\} \\ = - \frac {1}{a R} \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 R / a} (R a + a ^ {2}) - a ^ {2} \right], \\ \end{array}
$$

ep

$$
D = \frac {a}{R} - \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 R / a} \left(1 + \frac {a}{R}\right). \tag {8.48}
$$

对于 $X$ 类似有

$$
\begin{array}{l} X = a \left\langle \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1}\right) \right| \frac {1}{r _ {1}} \left| \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2}\right) \right\rangle \\ = \frac {2 \pi}{\pi a ^ {2}} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} r \mathrm {e} ^ {- r / a} \mathrm {d} r \left(\int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {1}{a} \sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2} - 2 r R \cos \theta}} \sin \theta \mathrm {d} \theta\right) \\ = \frac {2}{a ^ {2}} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} r \mathrm {e} ^ {- r / a} \mathrm {d} r \left(\frac {1}{r R} \int_ {| R - r |} ^ {R + r} \mathrm {e} ^ {- y / a} y \mathrm {d} y\right) \\ = \frac {2}{a ^ {2}} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} r \mathrm {e} ^ {- r / a} \mathrm {d} r \left\{- \frac {a}{r R} \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {- (R + r) / a} (R + r + a) - \mathrm {e} ^ {- | R - r | / a} (| R - r | + a) \right] \right\} \\ = - \frac {2}{a R} \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {- R / a} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {d} r \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 r / a} (R + r + a) - \mathrm {e} ^ {- R / a} \int_ {0} ^ {R} \mathrm {d} r (R - r + a) \right. \\ \left. - \mathrm {e} ^ {R / a} \int_ {R} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {d} r \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 r / a} (r - R + a) \right] \\ = - \frac {2}{a R} \left\{\mathrm {e} ^ {- R / a} \left[ \frac {a}{2} (R + a) + \left(\frac {a}{2}\right) ^ {2} \right] - \mathrm {e} ^ {- R / a} \left[ (R + a) R - \frac {R ^ {2}}{2} \right] \right. \\ - \mathrm {e} ^ {R / a} \left[ - \frac {a}{2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 r / a} (r - R + a) - \left(\frac {a}{2}\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 r / a} \right] \Bigg | _ {R} ^ {\infty} \\ = - \frac {2}{a R} \left\{\mathrm {e} ^ {- R / a} \left[ \frac {a}{2} (R + a) + \left(\frac {a}{2}\right) ^ {2} \right] - \mathrm {e} ^ {- R / a} \left[ (R + a) R - \frac {R ^ {2}}{2} \right] \right. \\ \left. - \mathrm {e} ^ {R / a} \left[ \frac {a ^ {2}}{2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 R / a} + \left(\frac {a}{2}\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 R / a} \right] \right\} \\ = - \frac {2}{a R} \mathrm {e} ^ {- R / a} \left(- \frac {a R}{2} - \frac {R ^ {2}}{2}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

即

$$
X = \mathrm {e} ^ {- R / a} \left(1 + \frac {R}{a}\right). \tag {8.49}
$$

串串习题 8.10 假设在试探波函数(方程 (8.38)) 中使用负号:

$$
\psi = A [ \psi_ {0} (r) - \psi_ {0} (r ^ {\prime}) ].
$$

对于该情况，不需做任何新的积分， (类比方程 (8.52)) 求解 $F \left( x \right)$ 并作图 证明:没有明显的结合态.①(由于变分原理只给出了一个上限 这并不能证明该状态下键不能发生，但它看起来确实不大有希望.)

解答试探披函数为

$$
\psi = A \left[ \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1}\right) - \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2}\right) \right].
$$

归一化求

$$
\begin{array}{l} 1 = \int | \psi | ^ {2} d ^ {3} r \\ = | A | ^ {2} \left[ \int | \psi_ {0} (r _ {1}) | ^ {2} d ^ {3} r + \int | \psi_ {0} (r _ {2}) | ^ {2} d ^ {3} r - 2 \int \psi_ {0} (r _ {1}) \psi_ {0} (r _ {2}) d ^ {3} r \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

同样地，前面两项积分是 ，第三项只是前面变为负号.仍然定义

$$
I \equiv \left\langle \psi_ {0} (r _ {1}) \mid \psi_ {0} (r _ {2}) \right\rangle = \frac {1}{\pi a ^ {3}} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {- (r _ {1} + r _ {2}) / a} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} r.
$$

于是，归一化结果为

$$
\left| A \right| ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2 (1 - I)}.
$$

与方程 相比，也只是在 $I$ 前面为负号.现在，在此试探披函数下求 $H$ 的期望值.显然，与方程(8.50) 相比，也只是在第二项中由 $D + X$ 变为了 $D - X$ 于是，类比方程 (8.50)，得到新试探波函数下的能量期望值为

$$
\langle H \rangle = \left[ 1 + 2 \frac {D - X}{1 - I} \right] E _ {1},
$$

其中的 $E _ { 1 }$ ,I,D, $X$ 和原来定义完全一样.将习题 8.9 计算的结果(方程 (8.48) (8.49)) 代入上式并考虑两个质子间的排斥能 $e ^ { 2 } / 4 \pi \varepsilon _ { 0 } R = - 2 a E _ { 1 } / R$ (方程 (8.51)) ，令 $R / a \equiv x$ 以 $- E _ { 1 }$ 为单位表示能量，困此，系统的总能量小于

$$
\begin{array}{l} F (x) = \frac {E _ {\mathrm {t o t}}}{- E _ {1}} = \frac {2 a}{R} - 1 - 2 \frac {D - X}{1 - I} \\ = \frac {2}{x} - 1 - 2 \frac {\frac {1}{x} - \left(1 + \frac {1}{x}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 x} - (1 + x) \mathrm {e} ^ {- x}}{1 - \left(1 + x + \frac {x ^ {2}}{3}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- x}} \\ = - 1 + \frac {2}{x} \left[ \frac {\left(1 + \frac {1}{x}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 x} + \left(- 1 + \frac {2 x ^ {2}}{3}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- x}}{1 - \left(1 + x + \frac {x ^ {2}}{3}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- x}} \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

![](images/bd2851346420bb19551ac0159912142318e737908cae481000fb5a8dc5ad832a.jpg)  
8.1

函数作图如图 所示(与原题中未变号的相比).

从图 8.1 中显然可以得到以下结论，新的试探披函数下的 $F \left( x \right)$ 没有极小值，恒大于一 ，也就是说系统，也能量恒大于 $E _ { 1 }$ 氢原子基态能量).因此对新的波函数，不存在束缚态.

*** 王宝 习题 8.11 $F \left( x \right)$ 在平衡点处的二阶导数可估算氢分子离子中两个质子振动的固有频率 $( \omega )$ ( 2.3 节).如果诙谐振子的基态能量( $( \hbar \omega / 2 )$ 超过体系的束缚能，它将会分离.证明:事实上振子能量足够小，不会发生这种情况，并估计有多少束缚的振动能级.注释你不可能得到最小值的位置，更不用说二阶导数的数值.需要在计算机上做数值运算.

解答由方程 (8.52)

$$
F (x) = - 1 + \frac {2}{x} \left\{\frac {\left[ 1 - (2 / 3) x ^ {2} \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {- x} + (1 + x) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 x}}{1 + \left[ 1 + x + (1 / 3) x ^ {2} \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {- x}} \right\},
$$

令一阶导数等于零，数值计算可得极值发生在 $x = 2 . 4 9 3$ ，此点的二阶导数为 $F ^ { \prime \prime } = 0 . 1 2 5 7$ 2.3可知谐振子势能的二阶导数与频率有关系，即

$$
m \omega^ {2} = V ^ {\prime \prime} = - \frac {E _ {1}}{a ^ {2}} F ^ {\prime \prime}.
$$

所以

$$
\omega = \frac {1}{a} \sqrt {\frac {0 . 1 2 5 7 E _ {1}}{m}}.
$$

这里 $m$ 为的化质量 $m = { \frac { m _ { p } m _ { p } } { m _ { p } + m _ { p } } } = { \frac { m _ { p } } { 2 } }$ mpmp mp 相关量代入，即可估算出氢分子离子中两个质子振动的固有频率

$$
\omega = \frac {1}{a} \sqrt {\frac {- E _ {1} F ^ {\prime \prime}}{m}} = 3. 4 2 \times 1 0 ^ {1 4} \mathrm {s} ^ {- 1}.
$$

于是得到基态振动能为

$$
\frac {1}{2} \hbar \omega = 0. 1 1 3 \mathrm {e V}.
$$

在此平衡位置 $x = 2 . 4 9 3$ ) $F = - 1 . 1 2 9 7 .$ 于是，束缚态能量为 $0 . 1 2 9 7 \times 1 3 . 6 ~ \mathrm { e V } = 1 . 7 6 ~ \mathrm { e V }$ 可见，其能量值远大于质子振动的基态能量 $\mathrm { ( 0 . 1 1 3 ~ e V ) }$ ).

通过

$$
E = (n + 1 / 2) \hbar \omega
$$

可求出束缚能级的数目，代入各个数值，求出当 $n = 7 . 2 9$ 时， $E = 1 . 7 6 ~ \mathrm { e V }$ ，这表明质子束缚振动态至少存在 个(包括基态).

习题 8.12 证明:反对称态(方程 (8.56)) 可以用 8.3 节的分子轨道来表示一一具体来说，通过将一个电子置于成键轨道(方程 (8.38)) 和一个电子置于反键轨道(方(8.53)).

证明略去归一化困子，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \psi_ {-} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}, \boldsymbol {r} _ {2}\right) = \left[ \psi_ {B} \left(r _ {1}\right) \psi_ {A} \left(r _ {2}\right) - \psi_ {A} \left(r _ {1}\right) \psi_ {B} \left(r _ {2}\right) \right] \\ = \left[ \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1}\right) + \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1} ^ {\prime}\right) \right] \left[ \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2}\right) - \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2} ^ {\prime}\right) \right] \\ - \left[ \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1}\right) - \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1} ^ {\prime}\right) \right] \left[ \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2}\right) + \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2} ^ {\prime}\right) \right] \\ = \psi_ {0} (r _ {1}) \psi_ {0} (r _ {2}) - \psi_ {0} (r _ {1}) \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2} ^ {\prime}\right) + \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1} ^ {\prime}\right) \psi_ {0} (r _ {2}) - \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1} ^ {\prime}\right) \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2} ^ {\prime}\right) \\ - \psi_ {0} (r _ {1}) \psi_ {0} (r _ {2}) - \psi_ {0} (r _ {1}) \psi_ {0} (r _ {2} ^ {\prime}) + \psi_ {0} (r _ {1} ^ {\prime}) \psi_ {0} (r _ {2}) + \psi_ {0} (r _ {1} ^ {\prime}) \psi_ {0} (r _ {2} ^ {\prime}) \\ = - 2 \left[ \psi_ {0} (r _ {1}) \psi_ {0} (r _ {2} ^ {\prime}) - \psi_ {0} (r _ {1} ^ {\prime}) \psi_ {0} (r _ {2}) \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

习题 8.13 验证电子一质子势能方程 (8.63).

证明披函数为

$$
\psi_ {\pm} \left(r _ {1}, r _ {2}\right) = A _ {\pm} \left[ \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1}\right) \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2} ^ {\prime}\right) \pm \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1} ^ {\prime}\right) \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2}\right) \right],
$$

则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle - \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r _ {1}} \right\rangle_ {\pm} = \langle \psi_ {\pm} (r _ {1}, r _ {2}) | \left(- \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r _ {1}}\right) | \psi_ {\pm} (r _ {1}, r _ {2}) \rangle \\ = - \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} a} A _ {\pm} ^ {2} \left[ \left\langle \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1}\right) \right| \frac {a}{r _ {1}} \left| \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1}\right) \right\rangle \left\langle \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2} ^ {\prime}\right) \right| \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2} ^ {\prime}\right) \right\rangle \\ + \left\langle \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1} ^ {\prime}\right) \right| \frac {a}{r _ {1}} \left| \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1} ^ {\prime}\right) \right\rangle \left\langle \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2}\right) \mid \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2}\right) \right\rangle \\ \left. \pm 2 \left\langle \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1}\right) \right| \frac {a}{r _ {1}} \left| \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1} ^ {\prime}\right) \right\rangle \left\langle \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2} ^ {\prime}\right) | \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2}\right) \right\rangle \right] \\ = - \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} a} \frac {1}{2 (1 \pm I ^ {2})} (1 \times 1 + D \times 1 \pm 2 \times I) \\ = - \frac {1}{2} \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} a} \frac {1 + D \pm 2 I X}{1 \pm I ^ {2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

串串串习题 8.14 在方程 (8.65) (8.66) 中定义了两体积分 $D _ { 2 }$ 和 $X _ { 2 }$ 为了计算$D _ { 2 }$ 可以写成如下形式:

$$
\begin{array}{l} D _ {2} = \int \left| \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {2} ^ {\prime}\right) \right| ^ {2} \varPhi (r _ {2}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} r _ {2} \\ = \iiint \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \sqrt {R ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 R r _ {2} \cos \theta_ {2}} / a}}{\pi a ^ {3}} \Phi (r _ {2}) r _ {2} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {2} \sin \theta_ {2} \mathrm {d} \theta_ {2} \mathrm {d} \phi_ {2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $\theta _ { 2 }$ 是 $\pmb { R }$ 和 $r _ { 2 }$ 之间的夹角(教材图 8.8) ，且

$$
\Phi \left(r _ {2}\right) \equiv \int \left| \psi_ {0} \left(r _ {1}\right) \right| ^ {2} \frac {a}{\left| \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} \right|} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1}.
$$

(a) 首先考虑 $\mathbf { \Delta } _ { \mathbf { r _ { 1 } } }$ 上的积分.取 $z$ 轴沿 $r _ { 2 }$ 方向(对于第一个积分而言， $r _ { 2 }$ 是一个常矢量)以便

$$
\varPhi (r _ {2}) = \frac {1}{\pi a ^ {3}} \iiint \frac {a \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 r _ {1} / a}}{\sqrt {r _ {1} ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 r _ {1} r _ {2} \cos \theta_ {1}}} r _ {1} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {1} \sin \theta_ {1} \mathrm {d} \theta_ {1} \mathrm {d} \phi_ {1}.
$$

先对角度积分，然后证明:

$$
\Phi \left(r _ {2}\right) = \frac {a}{r _ {2}} - \left(1 + \frac {a}{r _ {2}}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 r _ {2} / a}.
$$

(仙b) (a叫)中的结果代入 $D _ { 2 }$ 的关系中，并证明:

$$
D _ {2} = \frac {a}{R} - \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 R / a} \left[ \frac {1}{6} \left(\frac {R}{a}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {3}{4} \left(\frac {R}{a}\right) + \frac {1 1}{8} + \frac {a}{R} \right].
$$

同样先做角度积分

注释积分 $X _ { 2 }$ 也可以用闭合形式计算，但过程相当复杂.①我们只引用结果，

$$
\begin{array}{l} X _ {2} = \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 R / a} \left[ \frac {5}{8} - \frac {2 3}{2 0} \frac {R}{a} - \frac {3}{5} \left(\frac {R}{a}\right) ^ {2} - \frac {1}{1 5} \left(\frac {R}{a}\right) ^ {3} \right] \\ + \frac {6}{5} \frac {a}{R} I ^ {2} \left[ \gamma + \log \left(\frac {R}{a}\right) + \left(\frac {\tilde {I}}{I}\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {E i} \left(- \frac {4 R}{a}\right) - 2 \frac {\tilde {I}}{I} \mathrm {E i} \left(- \frac {2 R}{a}\right) \right], \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $\gamma = 0 . 5 7 7 2 \cdots$ 是欧拉常数， $\operatorname { E i } \left( x \right)$ 是指数积分

$$
\operatorname {E i} (x) = - \int_ {- x} ^ {\infty} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- t}}{t} \mathrm {d} t,
$$

通过改变 $R$ 的符号可以从 $I$ 中得到 $\tilde { I }$ :

$$
\tilde {I} = \mathrm {e} ^ {R / a} \left[ 1 - \frac {R}{a} + \frac {1}{3} \left(\frac {R}{a}\right) ^ {2} \right].
$$

解答 (a)

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Phi (r _ {2}) = \int | \psi_ {0} (r _ {1}) | ^ {2} \frac {a}{| \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} |} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} \\ = \iiint \left(\frac {1}{\sqrt {\pi a ^ {3}}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {r _ {1}}{a}}\right) ^ {2} \frac {a}{\sqrt {r _ {1} ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 r _ {1} r _ {2} \cos \theta_ {1}}} r _ {1} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {1} \sin \theta_ {1} \mathrm {d} \theta_ {1} \mathrm {d} \phi_ {1} \\ = \frac {1}{\pi a ^ {3}} \iint \frac {a e ^ {- \frac {2 r _ {1}}{a}}}{\sqrt {r _ {1} ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 r _ {1} r _ {2} \cos \theta_ {1}}} r _ {1} ^ {2} d r _ {1} \sin \theta_ {1} d \theta_ {1} \int_ {0} ^ {2 \pi} d \phi_ {1} \\ = \frac {2}{a ^ {2}} \iint \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {2 r _ {1}}{a}}}{\sqrt {r _ {1} ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 r _ {1} r _ {2} \cos \theta_ {1}}} r _ {1} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {1} \sin \theta_ {1} \mathrm {d} \theta_ {1} \\ = \frac {2}{a ^ {2}} \int \int \frac {\sin \theta}{\sqrt {r _ {1} ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 r _ {1} r _ {2} \cos \theta_ {1}}} \mathrm {d} \theta_ {1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {2 r _ {1}}{a}} r _ {1} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {1}. \\ \end{array}
$$

先对 $\theta _ { 1 }$ 积分:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \frac {\sin \theta_ {1}}{\sqrt {r _ {1} ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 r _ {1} r _ {2} \cos \theta_ {1}}} \mathrm {d} \theta_ {1} \\ = \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \frac {- 1}{\sqrt {r _ {1} ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 r _ {1} r _ {2} \cos \theta_ {1}}} d \cos \theta_ {1} \\ = \int_ {1} ^ {- 1} \frac {- 1}{\sqrt {r _ {1} ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 r _ {1} r _ {2} t}} d t \\ = \frac {1}{2 r _ {1} r _ {2}} \int_ {1} ^ {- 1} \left(r _ {1} ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 r _ {1} r _ {2} t\right) ^ {- \frac {1}{2}} d (- 2 r _ {1} r _ {2} t) \\ = \frac {1}{r _ {1} r _ {2}} \left(r _ {1} ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 r _ {1} r _ {2} t\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \Bigg | _ {1} ^ {- 1} \\ = \frac {1}{r _ {1} r _ {2}} \left(r _ {1} + r _ {2} - | r _ {1} - r _ {2} |\right) \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
= \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} \frac {2}{r _ {2}}, & (r _ {1} <   r _ {2}) \\ \frac {2}{r _ {1}}, & (r _ {1} > r _ {2}) \end{array} \right.
$$

所以

$$
\varPhi \left(r _ {2}\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l} \frac {2}{a ^ {2}} \int \frac {2}{r _ {2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {2 r _ {1}}{a}} r _ {1} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {1}, \quad \left(r _ {1} <   r _ {2}\right) \\ \frac {2}{a ^ {2}} \int 2 r _ {1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {2 r _ {1}}{a}} \mathrm {d} r _ {1}, \quad \left(r _ {1} > r _ {2}\right) \end{array} \right..
$$

再对 $r _ { 1 }$ 进行分段积分，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \varPhi (r _ {2}) = \frac {4}{a ^ {2}} \left(\frac {1}{r _ {2}} \int_ {0} ^ {r _ {2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {2 r _ {1}}{a}} r _ {1} \mathrm {d} r _ {1} + \int_ {r _ {2}} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {2 r _ {1}}{a}} r _ {1} \mathrm {d} r _ {1}\right) \\ = \frac {4}{a ^ {2}} \left\{\frac {1}{r _ {2}} \left[ - \frac {a ^ {3}}{4} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {2 r _ {1}}{a}} \left(\frac {2 r _ {1} ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} + \frac {2 r _ {1}}{a} + 1\right) \right] \Bigg | _ {0} ^ {r _ {2}} + \left[ - \frac {a ^ {2}}{4} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {2 r _ {1}}{a}} \left(\frac {2 r _ {1}}{a} + 1\right) \right] \Bigg | _ {r _ {2}} ^ {\infty} \right\} \\ = \frac {a}{r _ {2}} - \left(1 + \frac {a}{r _ {2}}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {2 r _ {2}}{a}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

(b) (a) 中的结果代入 $D _ { 2 }$ 中，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} D _ {2} = \frac {1}{\pi a ^ {3}} \int \int \mathrm {e} ^ {\frac {- 2 \sqrt {R ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 R r _ {2} \cos \theta_ {2}}}{a}} \left[ \frac {a}{r _ {2}} - \left(1 + \frac {a}{r _ {2}}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {2 r _ {2}}{a}} \right] \\ \cdot r _ {2} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {2} \sin \theta_ {2} \mathrm {d} \theta_ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {2 \pi} \mathrm {d} \phi_ {2} \\ = \frac {2 \pi}{\pi a ^ {3}} \int \int \mathrm {e} ^ {\frac {- 2 \sqrt {R ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 R r _ {2} \cos \theta_ {2}}}{a}} \left[ \frac {a}{r _ {2}} - \left(1 + \frac {a}{r _ {2}}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {2 r _ {2}}{a}} \right] r _ {2} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {2} \sin \theta_ {2} \mathrm {d} \theta_ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

先对 $\theta _ { 2 }$ 进行积分，

$$
I _ {\theta_ {2}} = \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \mathrm {e} ^ {\frac {- 2 \sqrt {R ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 R r _ {2} \cos \theta_ {2}}}{a}} \sin \theta_ {2} \mathrm {d} \theta_ {2},
$$

这里需要进行变量代换，

令 $u = { \frac { 2 \sqrt { R ^ { 2 } + r _ { 2 } ^ { 2 } - 2 R r _ { 2 } \cos \theta _ { 2 } } } { a } }$ =., . $\mathrm { d } u = \frac { 2 R r _ { 2 } \sin \theta _ { 2 } } { \sqrt { R ^ { 2 } + r _ { 2 } ^ { 2 } - 2 R r _ { 2 } \cos \theta _ { 2 } } } \frac { 1 } { a } \mathrm { d } \theta _ { 2 } .$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathrm {d} u}{u} = \frac {2 R r _ {2} \sin \theta_ {2}}{\sqrt {R ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 R r _ {2} \cos \theta_ {2}}} \frac {1}{a} \frac {a}{2 \sqrt {R ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 R r _ {2} \cos \theta_ {2}}} \mathrm {d} \theta_ {2} \\ = \frac {R r _ {2} \sin \theta_ {2}}{R ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 R r _ {2} \cos \theta_ {2}} \mathrm {d} \theta_ {2} \\ = \frac {\frac {4}{a ^ {2}} R r _ {2} \sin \theta_ {2}}{\left(\frac {2}{a} \sqrt {R ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 R r _ {2} \cos \theta_ {2}}\right) ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} \theta_ {2} \\ = \frac {4}{a ^ {2}} R r _ {2} \sin \theta_ {2} \frac {1}{u ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} \theta_ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以 $\sin \theta _ { 2 } \mathrm { d } \theta _ { 2 } = \frac { a ^ { 2 } } { 4 R r _ { 2 } } u \mathrm { d } u$ 一工-u4Rr2

困此

$$
I _ {\theta} = \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \mathrm {e} ^ {- u} \frac {a ^ {2}}{4 R r _ {2}} u \mathrm {d} u = \frac {a ^ {2}}{4 R r _ {2}} \int_ {u _ {1}} ^ {u _ {2}} u \mathrm {e} ^ {- u} \mathrm {d} u = - \frac {a ^ {2}}{4 R r _ {2}} (u + 1) \left. \mathrm {e} ^ {- u} \right| _ {u _ {1}} ^ {u _ {2}}.
$$

由 $u = \frac { 2 } { a } \sqrt { R ^ { 2 } + r _ { 2 } ^ { 2 } - 2 R r _ { 2 } \cos \theta _ { 2 } }$ 所以积分上、下限为

$$
u _ {2} = \frac {2}{a} \sqrt {R ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} + 2 R r _ {2}} = \frac {2}{a} (R + r _ {2}) = \frac {2 R}{a} \left(1 + \frac {r _ {2}}{R}\right),
$$

$$
u _ {1} = \frac {2}{a} \sqrt {R ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 R r _ {2}} = \frac {2}{a} | R - r _ {2} | = \frac {2 R}{a} \left| 1 - \frac {r _ {2}}{R} \right|.
$$

令 $\rho = \frac { 2 R } { a }$ . $x = \frac { r _ { 2 } } { R }$ ， ， 则

$$
u _ {2} = \rho (1 + x), \quad u _ {1} = \rho | 1 - x |.
$$

所以，

$$
I _ {\theta} = \frac {1}{\rho^ {2} x} \left\{\left[ \rho | 1 - x | + 1 \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {- \rho | 1 - x |} - \left[ \rho (1 + x) + 1 \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {- \rho (1 + x)} \right\}.
$$

再对 $r _ { 2 }$ 进行积分，即为对 $x$ 的积分.

$$
\begin{array}{l} D _ {2} = \frac {2 \pi}{\pi a ^ {3}} \int I _ {\theta} \left[ \frac {a}{r _ {2}} - \left(1 + \frac {a}{r _ {2}}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {\frac {- 2 r _ {2}}{a}} \right] r _ {2} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {2} \\ = \frac {1}{4} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \left\{\left[ \rho \left| 1 - x \right| + 1 \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {- \rho \left| 1 - x \right|} - \left[ \rho (1 + x) + 1 \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {- \rho (1 + x)} \right\} \\ \cdot \left[ 2 - (\rho x + 2) e ^ {- \rho x} \right] d x \\ = \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- \rho}}{4} \int_ {0} ^ {1} \left\{\left[ \rho (1 - x) + 1 \right] \rho^ {\rho x} \right\} [ 2 - (\rho x + 2) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \rho x} ] \mathrm {d} x \\ + \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\rho}}{4} \int_ {1} ^ {\infty} \left\{\left[ \rho (x - 1) + 1 \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {- \rho x} \right\} [ 2 - (\rho x + 2) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \rho x} ] \mathrm {d} x \\ - \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\rho}}{4} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \left\{\left[ \rho (1 + x) + 1 \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {- \rho x} \right\} [ 2 - (\rho x + 2) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \rho x} ] \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- \rho}}{4} \left\{2 \int_ {0} ^ {1} [ (\rho + 1) - \rho x ] \mathrm {e} ^ {\rho x} \mathrm {d} x - \int_ {0} ^ {1} [ 2 (\rho + 1) + (\rho - 1) \rho x - (\rho x) ^ {2} ] \mathrm {d} x \right\} \\ + \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\rho}}{4} \left\{2 \int_ {1} ^ {\infty} [ (1 - \rho) + \rho x ] \mathrm {e} ^ {- \rho x} \mathrm {d} x \right. \\ \left. - \int_ {1} ^ {\infty} [ 2 (1 - \rho) + \rho x (3 - \rho) + (\rho x) ^ {2} ] e ^ {- 2 \rho x} d x \right\} \\ - \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- \rho}}{4} \left\{2 \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} [ (1 + \rho) + \rho x ] \mathrm {e} ^ {- \rho x} \mathrm {d} x \right. \\ \left. - \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} [ 2 (1 + \rho) + (3 + \rho) \rho x + (\rho x) ^ {2} ] e ^ {- 2 \rho x} d x \right\} \\ = \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- \rho}}{4} \left[ \frac {2}{\rho} (2 \mathrm {e} ^ {\rho} - \rho - 2) - \left(2 + \frac {3}{2} \rho + \frac {1}{6} \rho^ {2}\right) \right] + \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\rho}}{4} \left[ \frac {4}{\rho} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \rho} - \left(\frac {3}{4} + \frac {2}{\rho}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \rho} \right] \\ - \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- \rho}}{4} \left[ 2 \left(1 + \frac {2}{\rho}\right) - \left(\frac {5}{4} + \frac {2}{\rho}\right) \right] \\ = \frac {2}{\rho} - \frac {1}{8} \left(1 1 + 3 \rho + \frac {1 6}{\rho} + \frac {\rho^ {2}}{3}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \rho}. \\ \end{array}
$$

再将 $\rho = \frac { 2 R } { a }$ 入上式中，得

$$
D _ {2} = \frac {a}{R} - \left(\frac {1 1}{8} + \frac {3 R}{4 a} + \frac {a}{R} + \frac {R ^ {2}}{6 a ^ {2}}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {2 R}{a}}.
$$

$\ast _ { c } < 0 _ { 3 }$ 习题 8.15 绘出 $H _ { 2 }$ 的单态和三重态的动能随 $R / a$ 的变化曲线.对电子-质子势能和电子一电子势能也做同样的事情.你会发现，对于所有的 $R$ 值，三重态的势能都比单态低.然而 由于单态的动能要小得多，所以它的总能量要低.注释 在没有大的动能消耗来调整自旋的情况下，例如原子中部分填充轨道中的两个电子 三重态的能量可能会更低.这就是洪德第一定则背后的物理原理

解答先分别给出所需要物理量的具体表达式，由方程 (8 .48) (8 .49) 知，

$$
D = \frac {a}{R} - \left(1 + \frac {a}{R}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 R / a}, \quad D = \frac {1}{\rho} - \left(1 + \frac {1}{\rho}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \rho};
$$

$$
X = \left(1 + \frac {R}{a}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- R / a}, \quad X = (1 + \rho) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \rho};
$$

其中 $\rho = \frac { R } { a }$

由方程 (8.43) 知，

$$
I = \mathrm {e} ^ {- R / a} \left[ 1 + \left(\frac {R}{a}\right) + \frac {1}{3} \left(\frac {R}{a}\right) ^ {2} \right] = \mathrm {e} ^ {- \rho} \left(1 + \rho + \frac {1}{3} \rho^ {2}\right);
$$

由方程 (8.70) (8.71) 知，

$$
\begin{array}{l} D _ {2} = \frac {a}{R} - \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 R / a} \left[ \frac {1}{6} \left(\frac {R}{a}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {3}{4} \left(\frac {R}{a}\right) + \frac {1 1}{8} + \frac {a}{R} \right] \\ = \frac {1}{\rho} - \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \rho} \frac {1}{\rho} \left(1 + \frac {1 1}{8} \rho + \frac {3}{4} \rho^ {2} + \frac {1}{6} \rho^ {3}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} X _ {2} = \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 R / a} \left[ \frac {5}{8} - \frac {2 3}{2 0} \frac {R}{a} - \frac {3}{5} \left(\frac {R}{a}\right) ^ {2} - \frac {1}{1 5} \left(\frac {R}{a}\right) ^ {3} \right] \\ + \frac {6}{5} \frac {a}{R} I ^ {2} \left[ \gamma + \log \left(\frac {R}{a}\right) + \left(\frac {\tilde {I}}{I}\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {E i} \left(- \frac {4 R}{a}\right) - 2 \frac {\tilde {I}}{I} \mathrm {E i} \left(- \frac {2 R}{a}\right) \right] \\ = \frac {1}{5} \left\{\mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \rho} \left[ \frac {2 5}{8} - \frac {2 3}{4 \rho} - 3 \rho^ {2} - \frac {1}{3} \rho^ {3} \right] \right. \\ \left. + \frac {6}{\rho} I [ \rho ] ^ {2} (\gamma + \log [ \rho ]) + I [ - \rho ] ^ {2} \operatorname {E i} [ - 4 \rho ] - 2 I [ \rho ] I [ - \rho ] \operatorname {E i} [ - 2 \rho ] \right\}, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $\gamma = 0 . 5 7 7 2 \cdots$ 为欧拉常数， $\operatorname { E i } \left( x \right)$ 为指数积分，则

$$
\operatorname {E i} (x) = - \int_ {- x} ^ {\infty} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- t}}{t} \mathrm {d} t,
$$

$\tilde { I }$ 为 $I$ 中改变 $R$ 的符号:

$$
\tilde {I} = \mathrm {e} ^ {R / a} \left[ 1 - \frac {R}{a} + \frac {1}{3} \left(\frac {R}{a}\right) ^ {2} \right].
$$

由方程 (8.68) 知，哈密顿量为

$$
\langle H \rangle_ {\pm} = 2 E _ {1} \left[ 1 - \frac {a}{R} + \frac {2 D - D _ {2} \pm (2 I X - X _ {2})}{1 \pm I ^ {2}} \right]
$$

$$
= - 2 \left\{1 - \frac {1}{\rho} + \left[ \frac {2 D [ \rho ] - D _ {2} [ \rho ] \pm (2 I [ \rho ] X [ \rho ] - X _ {2} [ \rho ])}{1 \pm I [ \rho ] ^ {2}} \right] \right\}.
$$

由方程 (8.62) 知，动能项为

$$
2 \left\langle - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \nabla_ {1} ^ {2} \right\rangle = 2 \left[ E _ {1} + \left(\frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} a}\right) \frac {1 \pm I X}{1 \pm I ^ {2}} \right] = - 2 \left[ 1 - 2 \frac {1 \pm I [ \rho ] X [ \rho ]}{1 \pm I [ \rho ] ^ {2}} \right].
$$

自方程 (8.63) 知，离子势能项为

$$
\begin{array}{l} 2 \left\langle - \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r _ {1}} \right\rangle = - \frac {2}{2} \left(\frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} a}\right) \frac {1 + D \pm 2 I X}{1 \pm I ^ {2}} \\ = - 4 \frac {1 + D [ \rho ] \pm 2 I [ \rho ] X [ \rho ]}{1 \pm I [ \rho ] ^ {2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

由方程 (8.6η 知，电子之间相互作用势为

$$
\left\langle V _ {e e} \right\rangle = \left(\frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} a}\right) \frac {D _ {2} \pm X _ {2}}{1 \pm I ^ {2}} = 2 \frac {D _ {2} [ \rho ] \pm X _ {2} [ \rho ]}{1 \pm I [ \rho ] ^ {2}}.
$$

程序及绘图(图 $8 . 2 \sim$ ，图 8.5) 如下:

# 补充习题

# 习题 8.16

(a) 使用函数 $\psi \left( x \right) = A x \left( a - x \right)$ ( $0 < x < a$ 范围内，否则为 0) 求在无限深方势阱中基态束缚能级的上限.  
(b) 对于某些实数 $p$ 推广到 $\psi \left( x \right) = A \left[ x \left( a - x \right) \right] ^ { p }$ 形式的函数 $p$ 的优化值是多少?基态能量的最佳上限是多少?和精确值作对比.答案 $\left( 5 + 2 { \sqrt { 6 } } \right) \hbar ^ { 2 } / ( 2 m a ^ { 2 } )$ .

解答 (a) 归一化波函数，求系数 $A$

$$
\begin{array}{l} I = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \psi^ {*} (x) \psi (x) d x = A ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {a} x ^ {2} \left(x ^ {2} - 2 a x + a ^ {2}\right) d x \\ = A ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {a} \left(x ^ {4} - 2 a x ^ {3} + a ^ {2} x ^ {2}\right) d x \\ = A ^ {2} \left(\frac {x ^ {5}}{5} - 2 a \frac {x ^ {4}}{4} + \frac {a ^ {2} x ^ {3}}{3}\right) \Bigg | _ {0} ^ {a} = A ^ {2} \left(\frac {a ^ {5}}{5} - \frac {a ^ {5}}{2} + \frac {a ^ {5}}{3}\right) \\ = A ^ {2} \frac {a ^ {5}}{3 0}, \\ \end{array}
$$

所以，

$$
A = \left(\frac {3 0}{a ^ {5}}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}}.
$$

能量期望值

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle H \rangle = \langle \psi | \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} | \psi \rangle = \frac {1}{2 m} \langle p \psi | p \psi \rangle \\ = \frac {1}{2 m} \int_ {0} ^ {a} \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d} \psi}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) ^ {*} \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d} \psi}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \int_ {0} ^ {a} \frac {\mathrm {d} \psi^ {*}}{\mathrm {d} x} \frac {\mathrm {d} \psi}{\mathrm {d} x} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2} A ^ {2}}{2 m} \int_ {0} ^ {a} \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} x} [ x (a - x) ] \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} x} [ x (a - x) ] \mathrm {d} x \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \frac {\hbar^ {2} A ^ {2}}{2 m} \int_ {0} ^ {a} (a - 2 x) (a - 2 x) d x \\ = - \frac {1}{2} \frac {\hbar^ {2} A ^ {2}}{2 m} \int_ {0} ^ {a} (a - 2 x) ^ {2} \mathrm {d} (a - 2 x) \\ = - \frac {1}{2} \frac {\hbar^ {2} A ^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {(a - 2 x) ^ {3}}{3} \Bigg | _ {0} ^ {a} \\ = - \frac {\hbar^ {2} A ^ {2} (- a) ^ {3}}{1 2 m} + \frac {\hbar^ {2} A ^ {2} a ^ {3}}{1 2 m} \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2} A ^ {2} a ^ {3}}{6 m} \\ = \frac {5 \hbar^ {2}}{m a ^ {2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此 $E _ { \mathrm { g s } } \leqslant \frac { 5 \hbar ^ { 2 } } { m a ^ { 2 } } = \frac { 1 0 } { \pi ^ { 2 } } \frac { \pi ^ { 2 } \hbar ^ { 2 } } { 2 m a ^ { 2 } } = \frac { 1 0 } { \pi ^ { 2 } } E _ { \mathrm { g s } }$ Eø.一----;; ma~

(b) 先归一化

$$
\begin{array}{l} 1 = \int_ {0} ^ {a} | \psi | ^ {2} d x \\ = A ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {a} x ^ {2 p} (a - x) ^ {2 p} d x \\ = A ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {a} \left(\frac {x}{a}\right) ^ {2 p} a ^ {2 p} \left(1 - \frac {x}{a}\right) ^ {2 p} a ^ {2 p} d \frac {x}{a} a \\ = A ^ {2} a ^ {4 p + 1} \int_ {0} ^ {a} \left(\frac {x}{a}\right) ^ {2 p} \left(1 - \frac {x}{a}\right) ^ {2 p} \mathrm {d} \frac {x}{a}, \\ \end{array}
$$

做变量代换 $u = { \frac { x } { a } }$ ，

$$
1 = A ^ {2} a ^ {4 p + 1} \int_ {0} ^ {1} u ^ {2 p} (1 - u) ^ {2 p} \mathrm {d} u.
$$

哈密顿量的期望值为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle H \rangle = \langle \psi | \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} | \psi \rangle = \frac {1}{2 m} \langle p \psi | p \psi \rangle \\ = \frac {1}{2 m} \int_ {0} ^ {a} \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d} \psi}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) ^ {*} \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d} \psi}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \int_ {0} ^ {a} \left(\frac {\mathrm {d} \psi}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) ^ {*} \left(\frac {\mathrm {d} \psi}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2} A ^ {2}}{2 m} \int_ {0} ^ {a} p [ x (a - x) ] ^ {p - 1} (a - 2 x) p [ x (a - x) ] ^ {p - 1} (a - 2 x) d x \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2} A ^ {2} p ^ {2}}{2 m} \int_ {0} ^ {a} (a - 2 x) ^ {2} [ x (a - x) ] ^ {2 p - 2} d x \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2} A ^ {2} p ^ {2}}{2 m} \int_ {0} ^ {a} \left(1 - 2 \frac {x}{a}\right) ^ {2} a ^ {2} \left[ \frac {x}{a} \left(1 - \frac {x}{a}\right) \right] ^ {2 p - 2} a ^ {4 p - 4} \mathrm {d} \frac {x}{a}. \\ \end{array}
$$

$u = { \frac { x } { a } }$ ， 则

$$
\langle H \rangle = \frac {\hbar^ {2} A ^ {2} p ^ {2}}{2 m} a ^ {4 p - 1} \int_ {0} ^ {1} (1 - 2 u) ^ {2} [ u (1 - u) ] ^ {2 p - 2} \mathrm {d} u
$$

$$
= \frac {p ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}} \frac {\int_ {0} ^ {1} (1 - 2 u) ^ {2} [ u (1 - u) ] ^ {2 p - 2} \mathrm {d} u}{\int_ {0} ^ {1} | u (1 - u) | ^ {2 p} \mathrm {d} u}.
$$

上式分子的积分化简，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {0} ^ {1} (1 - 2 u) ^ {2} [ u (1 - u) ] ^ {2 p - 2} \mathrm {d} u \\ = \frac {1}{2 p - 1} \int_ {0} ^ {1} (1 - 2 u) \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} u} [ u (1 - u) ] ^ {2 p - 1} \\ = \frac {1}{2 p - 1} 2 \int_ {0} ^ {1} [ u (1 - u) ] ^ {2 p - 1} \mathrm {d} u. \\ \end{array}
$$

代入 $\langle H \rangle$ 中,得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle H \rangle = \frac {2 p ^ {2}}{2 p - 1} \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}} \frac {\int_ {0} ^ {1} [ u (1 - u) ] ^ {2 p - 1} \mathrm {d} u}{\int_ {0} ^ {1} | u (1 - u) | ^ {2 p} \mathrm {d} u} \\ = \frac {2 p (4 p + 1)}{2 p - 1} \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

求极小值

$$
0 = \frac {\mathrm {d} \langle H \rangle}{\mathrm {d} p} = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}} \frac {1 6 p ^ {2} - 1 6 p - 2}{(2 p - 1) ^ {2}},
$$

$p$ 取正值 $p _ { \operatorname* { m i n } } = \frac { 2 + \sqrt { 6 } } { 4 }$ 因此,

$$
E _ {\mathrm {g s}} \leqslant \langle H \rangle_ {\mathrm {m i n}} = \frac {2 p (4 p + 1)}{2 p - 1} \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}} = \frac {5 + 2 \sqrt {6}}{\pi^ {2}} E _ {\mathrm {g s}}.
$$

# 习题 8.17

(a）用以下形式的试探波函数：

$$
\psi (x) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} A \cos (\pi x / a), & - a / 2 <   x <   a / 2, \\ 0, & \text {其 他}, \end{array} \right.
$$

求一维简谐振子的基态束缚能. $a$ 的“最优”值是多少？将 $\langle H \rangle _ { \mathrm { m i n } }$ 与准确值作对比.注释该试探波函数在 $\pm a / 2$ 处有一个“扭结”(不连续导数)；你是否需要像在例题8.3 中所做的那样来考虑这一点？

(b)在区间 $( - a , a )$ 中取 $\psi \left( x \right) = B \sin \left( \pi x / a \right)$ ，求第一激发态束缚能上限，并与准确结果作对比.

解答(a）首先归一化试探波函数

$$
1 = \int | \psi | ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x = | A | ^ {2} \int_ {- a / 2} ^ {a / 2} \cos^ {2} \left(\frac {\pi x}{a}\right) \mathrm {d} x = | A | ^ {2} \frac {a}{2},
$$

得

$$
A = \sqrt {\frac {2}{a}}.
$$

对于一维筒谐振子，

$$
H = T + V = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} + \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} x ^ {2}.
$$

$$
\langle T \rangle = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \psi \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} \psi}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left(\frac {\pi}{a}\right) ^ {2} \int_ {- a / 2} ^ {a / 2} \psi^ {2} \mathrm {d} x = \frac {\pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}},
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle V \rangle = \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} x ^ {2} \psi^ {2} \mathrm {d} x = \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} \frac {2}{a} \int_ {- a / 2} ^ {a / 2} x ^ {2} \cos^ {2} \left(\frac {\pi x}{a}\right) \mathrm {d} x = \frac {m \omega^ {2} a ^ {2}}{4 \pi^ {2}} \left(\frac {\pi^ {2}}{6} - 1\right), \\ \langle H \rangle = \frac {\pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}} + \frac {m \omega^ {2} a ^ {2}}{4 \pi^ {2}} \left(\frac {\pi^ {2}}{6} - 1\right). \\ \frac {\partial}{\partial a} \left\langle H \right\rangle = - \frac {\pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{m a ^ {3}} + \frac {m \omega^ {2} a}{2 \pi^ {2}} \left(\frac {\pi^ {2}}{6} - 1\right) = 0 \Rightarrow a = \pi \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{m \omega}} \left(\frac {2}{\pi^ {2} / 6 - 1}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \\ \Rightarrow \left\langle H \right\rangle_ {\mathrm {m i n}} = \frac {1}{2} \hbar \omega \sqrt {\frac {\pi^ {2}}{3} - 2} = \frac {1}{2} \hbar \omega \cdot (1. 1 3 6) > \frac {1}{2} \hbar \omega . \\ \end{array}
$$

(波函数的工阶导数在 $x = \pm a / 2$ 处的确为 $\delta$ 函数，但是在此处的被函数为 ，放在运算中无需考虑.)

(b) 试探监函数为奇函数，它与基态波函数(偶函数)正交，所以可以由变分法求第一激发态能量的上限.归一化被函数

$$
1 = \int | \psi | ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x = | B | ^ {2} \int_ {- a} ^ {a} \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {\pi x}{a}\right) \mathrm {d} x = | B | ^ {2} a \Rightarrow B = \sqrt {\frac {1}{a}}.
$$

$$
\langle T \rangle = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \psi \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} \psi}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left(\frac {\pi}{a}\right) ^ {2} \int_ {- a} ^ {a} \psi^ {2} \mathrm {d} x = \frac {\pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}}.
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle V \rangle = \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} x ^ {2} \psi^ {2} \mathrm {d} x = \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} \frac {1}{a} \int_ {- a} ^ {a} x ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {\pi x}{a}\right) \mathrm {d} x = \frac {m \omega^ {2} a ^ {2}}{4 \pi^ {2}} \left(\frac {2 \pi^ {2}}{3} - 1\right). \\ \langle H \rangle = \frac {\pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}} + \frac {m \omega^ {2} a ^ {2}}{4 \pi^ {2}} \left(\frac {2 \pi^ {2}}{3} - 1\right). \\ \frac {\partial}{\partial a} \langle H \rangle = - \frac {\pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{m a ^ {3}} + \frac {m \omega^ {2} a}{2 \pi^ {2}} \left(\frac {2 \pi^ {2}}{3} - 1\right) = 0 \Rightarrow a = \pi \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{m \omega}} \left(\frac {2}{2 \pi^ {2} / 3 - 1}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \\ \Rightarrow \langle H \rangle_ {\mathrm {m i n}} = \frac {1}{2} \hbar \omega \sqrt {\frac {4 \pi^ {2}}{3} - 2} = \frac {1}{2} \hbar \omega \cdot (3. 3 4 1) > \frac {3}{2} \hbar \omega . \\ \end{array}
$$

# 事*习题 8.18

(a) 推广习题 8.2，对任意 $n$ 使用如下试探波函数①

$$
\psi (x) = \frac {A}{(x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}) ^ {n}},
$$

部分答案:b 的最优值由下式给出

$$
b ^ {2} = \frac {\hbar}{m \omega} \left[ \frac {n (4 n - 1) (4 n - 3)}{2 (2 n + 1)} \right] ^ {1 / 2}.
$$

(b) 求简谐振子第一激发态能量最小上限，取试探波函数为

$$
\psi (x) = \frac {B x}{(x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}) ^ {n}}.
$$

部分答案: $b$ 的最佳值由下式给出

$$
b ^ {2} = \frac {\hbar}{m \omega} \left[ \frac {n (4 n - 5) (4 n - 3)}{2 (2 n + 1)} \right] ^ {1 / 2}.
$$

(c) 注意到当 $n \to \infty$ 时，上限值趋于能量精确值.为什么会是这样?提示$n = 2$ ; $n = 3$ 和 $n = 4$ 的试探波函数分别作图，并将它们与真实波函数(方程 (2.60)(2.63)) 作对比具体分析从下面等式开始

$$
\mathrm {e} ^ {z} = \lim  _ {n \rightarrow \infty} \left(1 + \frac {z}{n}\right) ^ {n}.
$$

解答 (时归一化试探披函数

$$
1 = 2 | A | ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{\left(x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}\right) ^ {2 n}} \mathrm {d} x = \frac {| A | ^ {2}}{b ^ {4 n - 1}} \frac {\Gamma \left(\frac {1}{2}\right) \Gamma \left(\frac {4 n - 1}{2}\right)}{\Gamma (2 n)},
$$

得

$$
A = \sqrt {\frac {b ^ {4 n - 1} \Gamma (2 n)}{\Gamma \left(\frac {1}{2}\right) \Gamma \left(\frac {4 n - 1}{2}\right)}},
$$

其中利用了定积分公式

$$
\int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {x ^ {k}}{\left(x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}\right) ^ {l}} \mathrm {d} x = \frac {1}{2 b ^ {2 l - k - 1}} \frac {\Gamma \left(\frac {k + 1}{2}\right) \Gamma \left(\frac {2 l - k - 1}{2}\right)}{\Gamma (l)}.
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle T \rangle = \frac {1}{2 m} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \psi^ {*} \hat {p} ^ {2} \psi \mathrm {d} x = \frac {1}{2 m} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} (\hat {p} \psi) ^ {*} \hat {p} \psi \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \left(\frac {\mathrm {d} \psi}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) ^ {*} \frac {\mathrm {d} \psi}{\mathrm {d} x} \mathrm {d} x = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} | A | ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \left[ \frac {- 2 n x}{(x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}) ^ {n + 1}} \right] ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {2 n ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{m} | A | ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {x ^ {2}}{\left(x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}\right) ^ {2 n + 2}} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {2 n ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{m} \frac {b ^ {4 n - 1} \Gamma (2 n)}{\Gamma \left(\frac {1}{2}\right) \Gamma \left(\frac {4 n - 1}{2}\right)} \frac {\Gamma \left(\frac {2 + 1}{2}\right) \Gamma \left[ \frac {2 (2 n + 2) - 2 - 1}{2} \right]}{b ^ {2 (2 n + 2) - 2 - 1} \Gamma (2 n + 2)} \\ = \frac {2 n ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{m} \frac {b ^ {4 n - 1} \Gamma (2 n)}{\Gamma \left(\frac {1}{2}\right) \Gamma \left(\frac {4 n - 1}{2}\right)} \frac {\Gamma \left(\frac {3}{2}\right) \Gamma \left(\frac {4 n + 1}{2}\right)}{b ^ {4 n + 1} \Gamma (2 n + 2)} \\ = \frac {2 n ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{m} \frac {b ^ {4 n - 1} (2 n - 1) !}{\Gamma \left(\frac {1}{2}\right) \Gamma \left(\frac {4 n - 1}{2}\right)} \frac {\frac {1}{2} \Gamma \left(\frac {1}{2}\right) \left(2 n - \frac {1}{2}\right) \Gamma \left(\frac {4 n - 1}{2}\right)}{b ^ {4 n + 1} (2 n + 1) !} \\ = \frac {2 n ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{m b ^ {2}} \frac {\frac {1}{2} \left(2 n - \frac {1}{2}\right)}{(2 n + 1) \cdot 2 n} \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
= \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{4 m b ^ {2}} \frac {n (4 n - 1)}{2 n + 1}.
$$

上式积分运算利用了 $\left( \Gamma \left( z + 1 \right) = z \Gamma \left( z \right) ; \ \Gamma \left( n + 1 \right) = n ! \right.$ )

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle V \rangle = \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} x ^ {2} | \psi | ^ {2} d x = \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} 2 | A | ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {x ^ {2}}{\left(x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}\right) ^ {2 n}} d x \\ = m \omega^ {2} \frac {b ^ {4 n - 1} \Gamma (2 n)}{\Gamma \left(\frac {1}{2}\right) \Gamma \left(\frac {4 n - 1}{2}\right)} \frac {\Gamma \left(\frac {3}{2}\right) \Gamma \left(\frac {4 n - 3}{2}\right)}{2 b ^ {4 n - 3} \Gamma (2 n)} \\ = m \omega^ {2} b ^ {2} \frac {1}{\Gamma \left(\frac {1}{2}\right) \left(2 n - \frac {3}{2}\right) \Gamma \left(\frac {4 n - 3}{2}\right)} \frac {\frac {1}{2} \Gamma \left(\frac {1}{2}\right) \Gamma \left(\frac {4 n - 3}{2}\right)}{2} \\ = \frac {m \omega^ {2} b ^ {2}}{8 n - 6}. \\ \end{array}
$$

由 $\left. H \right. = { \frac { \hbar ^ { 2 } } { 4 m b ^ { 2 } } } { \frac { n \left( 4 n - 1 \right) } { 2 n + 1 } } + { \frac { m \omega ^ { 2 } b ^ { 2 } } { 8 n - 6 } } , { \frac { \partial \left. H \right. } { \partial b } } = - { \frac { \hbar ^ { 2 } } { 2 m b ^ { 3 } } } { \frac { n \left( 4 n - 1 \right) } { 2 n + 1 } } + { \frac { m \omega ^ { 2 } b } { 4 n - 3 } } = 0 ,$ ñ,2 n(4侃一 1) ，得

$$
b ^ {2} = \frac {\hbar}{m \omega} \left[ \frac {n (4 n - 1) (4 n - 3)}{2 (2 n + 1)} \right] ^ {1 / 2},
$$

所以，

$$
\langle H \rangle_ {\min } = \frac {1}{2} \hbar \omega \sqrt {\frac {2 n (4 n - 1)}{(2 n + 1) (4 n - 3)}} > \frac {1}{2} \hbar \omega .
$$

显然 →∞， (HLU $n \to \infty$ $ H  _ { \mathrm { m i n } } \underset { n  \infty } {  } \frac 1 2 \hbar \omega$

(b) $\psi \left( x \right)$ 为奇函数与基态披函数(偶函数)正交，所以可由变分法求第一激发态能量上限.归一化披函数

$$
1 = 2 \left| B \right| ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {x ^ {2}}{\left(x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}\right) ^ {2 n}} \mathrm {d} x = 2 \left| B \right| ^ {2} \frac {\Gamma \left(\frac {3}{2}\right) \Gamma \left(\frac {4 n - 3}{2}\right)}{2 b ^ {4 n - 3} \Gamma (2 n)},
$$

得

$$
B = \sqrt {\frac {b ^ {4 n - 3} \Gamma (2 n)}{\Gamma \left(\frac {3}{2}\right) \Gamma \left(\frac {4 n - 3}{2}\right)}}.
$$

中同样的方法，求得第一激发态能量期望值为

$$
\langle H \rangle = \langle T \rangle + \langle V \rangle = \frac {3 \hbar^ {2}}{4 m b ^ {2}} \frac {n (4 n - 3)}{2 n + 1} + \frac {3 m \omega^ {2} b ^ {2}}{2 (4 n - 5)}.
$$

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial b} \left<   H \right> = - \frac {3 \hbar^ {2}}{2 m b ^ {3}} \frac {n (4 n - 3)}{(2 n + 1)} + \frac {3 m \omega^ {2} b}{(4 n - 5)} = 0, \quad b ^ {2} = \frac {\hbar}{m \omega} \left[ \frac {n (4 n - 3) (4 n - 5)}{2 (2 n + 1)} \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}},
$$

得

$$
\left\langle H \right\rangle_ {\min } = \frac {3}{2} \hbar \omega \sqrt {\frac {2 n (4 n - 3)}{(2 n + 1) (4 n - 5)}} > \frac {3}{2} \hbar \omega .
$$

显然 →∞， (HM $n \to \infty$ $ H  _ { \mathrm { m i n } } \underset { n  \infty } {  } \frac { 3 } { 2 } \hbar \omega$

(c) 为什么当" $n \to \infty$ ， $\langle H \rangle _ { \mathrm { m i n } }$ 成为精确的能量值?由 (a) 的最优值表达式知，

$$
b ^ {2} = \frac {\hbar}{m \omega} \left[ \frac {n (4 n - 1) (4 n - 3)}{2 (2 n + 1)} \right] \stackrel {{\frac {1}{2}}} {\rightarrow} \underset {n \rightarrow \infty} {\frac {2 n \hbar}{m \omega}}.
$$

于是，试探波函数在 $n \to \infty$ 的情况下有

$$
\psi \left(x\right) = \frac {A}{\left(x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}\right) ^ {n}} = \frac {A}{b ^ {2 n} \left(1 + \frac {x ^ {2}}{b ^ {2}}\right) ^ {n}} \underset {n \rightarrow \infty} {\rightarrow} \frac {A}{b ^ {2 n} \left(1 + \frac {m \omega x ^ {2}}{2 \hbar} \frac {1}{n}\right) ^ {n}} \underset {n \rightarrow \infty} {\rightarrow} \frac {A}{b ^ {2 n}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega x ^ {2}}{2 \hbar}}.
$$

利用斯特林公式，对较大的 $z$ 有 $\Gamma \left( z + 1 \right) \approx z ^ { z } \mathrm { e } ^ { - z }$ ，则

$$
\begin{array}{l} A ^ {2} = \frac {b ^ {4 n - 1} \Gamma (2 n)}{\Gamma \left(\frac {1}{2}\right) \Gamma \left(\frac {4 n - 1}{2}\right)} \approx \frac {b ^ {4 n - 1} (2 n - 1) ^ {2 n - 1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- (2 n - 1)}}{\sqrt {\pi} \left(2 n - \frac {3}{2}\right) ^ {2 n - \frac {3}{2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- (2 n - \frac {3}{2})}} \\ = \frac {b ^ {4 n - 1}}{\sqrt {\pi} \sqrt {\mathrm {e}}} \left(\frac {2 n - 1}{2 n - \frac {3}{2}}\right) ^ {2 n - 1} \sqrt {2 n - \frac {3}{2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

由于

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(\frac {2 n - 1}{2 n - \frac {3}{2}}\right) ^ {2 n - 1} = \left(1 + \frac {\frac {1}{2}}{2 n - \frac {3}{2}}\right) ^ {2 n} \left(1 + \frac {\frac {1}{2}}{2 n - \frac {3}{2}}\right) ^ {- 1} \\ \underset {n \to \infty} {\approx} \left(1 + \frac {1}{4 n}\right) ^ {2 n} \frac {1}{1 + \frac {1}{4 n}} \underset {n \to \infty} {\rightarrow} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {\frac {1}{4}}\right) ^ {2} \approx \sqrt {\mathrm {e}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

所以，

$$
\begin{array}{l} A = \sqrt {\frac {b ^ {4 n - 1}}{\sqrt {\pi} \sqrt {\mathrm {e}}}} \left(\frac {2 n - 1}{2 n - \frac {3}{2}}\right) ^ {2 n - 1} \sqrt {2 n - \frac {3}{2}} \underset {n \rightarrow \hbar} {\rightarrow} \sqrt {\frac {b ^ {4 n - 1}}{\sqrt {\pi}} \sqrt {2 n}} = \left(\frac {2 n}{\pi}\right) ^ {2} b ^ {2 n - \frac {1}{2}} \frac {A}{b ^ {2 n}} \\ \underset {n \to \infty} {\rightarrow} \left(\frac {2 n}{\pi}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \frac {1}{b} = \left(\frac {2 n}{\pi}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \frac {1}{[ 2 n \hbar / (m \omega) ] ^ {\frac {1}{4}}} = \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{4}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

故

$$
\psi \left(x\right) = \underset {n \rightarrow \infty} {\rightarrow} \frac {A}{b ^ {2 n}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega x ^ {2}}{2 \hbar}} = \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{4}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega x ^ {2}}{2 \hbar}}.
$$

即波函数形式为精确的基态波函数，由此波函数得到的基态能量为精确基态能量也就不奇怪了.对第一激发态，同样有

$$
b ^ {2} = \frac {\hbar}{m \omega} \left[ \frac {n (4 n - 3) (4 n - 5)}{2 (2 n + 1)} \right] \underset {n \to \infty} {\overset {1} {\rightarrow}} \frac {2 n \hbar}{m \omega}.
$$

试探波函数

$$
\psi_ {1} (x) = \frac {B x}{(x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}) ^ {n}} = \frac {B x}{b ^ {2 n} \left(1 + \frac {x ^ {2}}{b ^ {2}}\right) ^ {n}} = \frac {B x}{b ^ {2 n} \left(1 + \frac {m \omega x ^ {2}}{2 \hbar n}\right) ^ {n}} \underset {n \rightarrow \infty} {\rightarrow} \frac {B}{b ^ {2 n}} x e ^ {- \frac {m \omega x ^ {2}}{2 \hbar}}.
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} B ^ {2} = \frac {b ^ {4 n - 3} \Gamma (2 n)}{\Gamma \left(\frac {3}{2}\right) \Gamma \left(\frac {4 n - 3}{2}\right)} = \frac {b ^ {4 n - 3} (2 n - 1) ^ {2 n - 1} e ^ {- (2 n - 1)}}{\frac {1}{2} \sqrt {\pi} \left(2 n - \frac {5}{2}\right) ^ {2 n - \frac {5}{2}} e ^ {- (2 n - \frac {5}{2})}} \\ = \frac {2 b ^ {4 n - 3}}{\sqrt {\pi} \mathrm {e} ^ {\frac {3}{2}}} \left(\frac {2 n - 1}{2 n - \frac {5}{2}}\right) ^ {2 n - 1} \left(2 n - \frac {5}{2}\right) ^ {\frac {3}{2}} \left(\frac {2 n - 1}{2 n - \frac {5}{2}}\right) ^ {2 n - 1} \\ = \left(1 + \frac {\frac {3}{2}}{2 n - \frac {5}{2}}\right) ^ {2 n} \left(1 + \frac {\frac {3}{2}}{2 n - \frac {5}{2}}\right) ^ {- 1} \underset {n \rightarrow \infty} {\approx} \left(1 + \frac {3}{4 n}\right) ^ {2 n} \frac {1}{1 + \frac {1}{n}} \approx \mathrm {e} ^ {\frac {3}{2}} \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {B}{b ^ {2 n}} \underset {n \to \infty} {\rightarrow} \frac {1}{b ^ {2 n}} \sqrt {\frac {2 b ^ {4 n - 3}}{\sqrt {\pi}} (2 n) ^ {\frac {3}{2}}} = \sqrt {\frac {2 b ^ {- 3}}{\sqrt {\pi}} (2 n) ^ {\frac {3}{2}}} = \sqrt {\frac {2}{\sqrt {\pi}} \left(\frac {m \omega}{2 n \hbar}\right) ^ {\frac {3}{2}} (2 n) ^ {\frac {3}{2}}} \\ = \sqrt {\frac {2}{\sqrt {\pi}} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) ^ {\frac {3}{2}}} = \sqrt {2} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{4}} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\psi_ {1} \left(x\right) \underset {n \rightarrow \infty} {\rightarrow} \frac {B}{b ^ {2 n}} x \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega x ^ {2}}{2 \hbar}} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{4}} \left[ 2 \left(\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} x \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega x ^ {2}}{2 \hbar}}.
$$

这正是精确的第一激发态披函数，所以由它可以得到精确的第一激发态能量.

8.6(a) (b) 给出了 $n = 2$ , 3, 时，本题所给的基态和第一激发态波函数，可以看出它们已经非常接近精确的基态和第一激发态披函数.图 8.6(a) 中虚结为基态试探披函数，按 点处的值从高到低依次为 $n = 2$ 的情况.实结为真实的基态披函数.圄 中虚结为第一激发态试探披函数，按极值从低到高依次为 $n = 2$ 的情况.实绩为真实的第一激发态波函数.
