# 10 章散 射

![](images/d793b6a331fd059c0fc861341ae6614cd7e17ea920806e71afcde6c3330b4a99.jpg)

# 本章主要内容概要

# 1.依勘t6J..毒根舍

微分散射截面:入射到横截面积 $\mathrm { d } \sigma$ 微元，内的拉子被散射到相应的微元立体角dn 内，其比例系数 $D \left( \theta , \phi \right) \equiv \mathrm { d } \sigma / \mathrm { d } \varOmega$ 称为微分散射截面

总散射截面: $\textstyle { \boldsymbol { \sigma } } = \int D \left( \theta , \phi \right) \mathrm { d } { \mathcal { \varOmega } }$

# 2. 量~惟射锺恪

设入射波沿 $z$ 方向传播，散射势是球对称势，则在远离散射中心的地方，波函数(入射波 $^ +$ 散射波)可以表示为

$$
\psi (r, \theta) \approx A \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k z} + f (\theta) \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}}{r} \right].
$$

$f \left( \theta \right)$ 是散射波振幅，微分散射截面与 $f \left( \theta \right)$ 关系为 $D \left( \theta \right) = \left| f \left( \theta \right) \right| ^ { 2 }$ •

显然，微分散射截面等于散射振幅绝对值的平方.散射振幅可以采用分波法和玻息近似两种方法计算.

# 3. i' 依射<<i1

分波法只适用于局域势场的情况，因此不适用于长程的库仓势.

![](images/f78d710ad396a89b29648be0c4f5b5dbabfa211bdf5f5f05b87e070efdc97489.jpg)  
10.1

假定球对称势场如圆 10.1 所示.

局域势的散射:散射区(较暗的阴影);中间区，这里 $V = 0$ (较亮的阴影);辐射区(此区域内 $k r \gg 1 \mathrm { \Omega } _ { , }$ ).

球对称势 $V ( r )$ 的薛定诗方程的波函数可以分离变量，即 $\psi ( r , \theta , \varphi ) = R ( r ) Y _ { l } ^ { m } ( \theta , \varphi )$ ，其中$Y _ { l } ^ { m }$ 是球谐函数， $\boldsymbol { u } ( \boldsymbol { r } ) = \boldsymbol { r } \boldsymbol { R } ( \boldsymbol { r } )$ 满足径向方程

$$
- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} u}{\mathrm {d} r ^ {2}} + \left[ V (r) + \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {l (l + 1)}{r ^ {2}} \right] u = E u.
$$

当 $r$ 很大时，势趋于零，且离心部分贡献可以忽略，有 $\frac { \mathrm { d } ^ { 2 } u } { \mathrm { d } r ^ { 2 } } \approx - k ^ { 2 } u$

其通解为

$$
u (r) = C \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r} + D \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k r};
$$

项代表出射球面波，第 项代表入射波.对于散射泼的求解，我们希望 $D = 0$

当 $r$ 很大时，有 $R ( r ) \sim { \frac { \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k r } } { r } }$ ~ikr

当 $r$ 处于中间区域时，外势基本为零 $\left( V \cong 0 \right)$ 时，但离心项要保留，薛定诗方程为${ \frac { \mathrm { d } ^ { 2 } u } { \mathrm { d } r ^ { 2 } } } - { \frac { l ( l + 1 ) } { r ^ { 2 } } } u = - k ^ { 2 } u$ ，通解为球贝塞尔函数的线性组合 $u ( r ) = A r j _ { l } ( k r ) + B r n _ { l } ( k r )$ .然而，无论 $j _ { l }$ 有点像正弦函数)还是 $n _ { l }$ 像一种广义的余弦函数)都不能表示出射波(或入射波).我们需要的是类似于 $\mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k r }$ 和 $\mathrm { e } ^ { - \mathrm { i } k r }$ 的线性组合;因此选择球汉克尔函数.

在 $r$ 很大时 $h _ { l } ^ { ( 1 ) } ( k r )$ 趋于 $\mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k r } / r$ ，而 $h _ { l } ^ { ( 2 ) } ( k r )$ 趋于 $\mathrm { e } ^ { - \mathrm { i } k r } / r$ ; 对于出射波，我们需要第一类球形汉克尔函数 $R ( r ) \sim h _ { l } ^ { ( 1 ) } ( k r )$ .

外部区域 $V ( r ) = 0 \nonumber$ ) 中的精确波函数为

$$
\psi (r, \theta , \varphi) = A \left\{\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k z} + \sum_ {l, m} C _ {l, m} h _ {l} ^ {(1)} (k r) Y _ {l} ^ {m} (\theta , \varphi) \right\}.
$$

项是入射平面波，求和项(展开系数 $C _ { l , m } )$ 是散射波.由于势是球对称的，波函数仅对应有 m=O 的项存在，有咛例 $m = 0$ $Y _ { l } ^ { 0 } ( \theta , \phi ) = { \sqrt { \frac { 2 l + 1 } { 4 \pi } } } P _ { l } ( \cos \theta ) .$

通常会重新定义展开系数 $( C _ { l , 0 } \equiv \mathrm { i } ^ { l + 1 } k \sqrt { 4 \pi ( 2 l + 1 ) } a _ { l } )$ ，得

$$
\psi (r, \theta) = A \left\{\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k z} + k \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {i} ^ {l + 1} (2 l + 1) a _ {l} h _ {l} ^ {(1)} (k r) P _ {l} (\cos \theta) \right\},
$$

$a _ { l }$ 称为第 $l$ 个分波振幅.

称为 $r$ 个分波振幅.大时，汉克尔函数 $\mathrm { ( - i ) } ^ { l + 1 } \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k r } / k r$ $\psi ( r , \theta ) \approx A \bigg \{ \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k z } + f ( \theta ) \frac { \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k r } } { r } \bigg \} ,$ 其中 $f ( \theta ) = \sum _ { l = 0 } ^ { \infty } ( 2 l + 1 ) a _ { l } P _ { l } ( \cos \theta ) .$

微分散射截面是

$$
D (\theta) = | f (\theta) | ^ {2} = \sum_ {l} \sum_ {l ^ {\prime}} (2 l + 1) (2 l ^ {\prime} + 1) a _ {l} ^ {*} a _ {l ^ {\prime}} P _ {l} (\cos \theta) P _ {l ^ {\prime}} (\cos \theta),
$$

总散射截面是

$$
\sigma = 4 \pi \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {\infty} (2 l + 1) \left| a _ {l} \right| ^ {2}.
$$

# 4.相移的思想

考虑在半轴 $x < 0$ 中局域势 $V ( x )$ 的一维散射(见圈 10.2)，在 $x = 0$ 处放直一堵"砖墙飞从左边入射的波

$$
\psi_ {i} (x) = A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} \quad (x <   - a)
$$

被完全反射

$$
\psi_ {r} (x) = B \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x} \qquad (x <   - a).
$$

![](images/98c35752b4c13de81fba8a9cdf68dedad3fd51eabed26f35836eef7ef5ec9e80.jpg)  
局域势的一维散射，该势位于一无限高墙的左侧

无论在相互作用区域 $( - a < x < 0 )$ 发生什么，由于几率守恒，反射波的振幅一定和入射波的振幅相等 $\langle \left| B \right| = \left| A \right| ,$ . 但它们的相位未必相同

如果完全没有势，仅在 $x = 0$ 处有一堵"精"，由于入射波函数和反射波函数之和在

原点处必须等于零，那么 $B = - A$

$$
\psi_ {0} (x) = A \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} - \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x}\right) \quad (V (x) = 0).
$$

如果势不为零，波函数(对于 $x < - a _ { , }$ 取如下形式:

$$
\psi (x) = A \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} - \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} (2 \delta - k x)}\right) \quad (V (x) \neq 0).
$$

三维情况下，入射平面注 $A \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k z }$ 在 $z$ 方向上没有角动量(瑞利公式不包含 $m \neq 0$ 的项)，但是它包含总角动量的所有值 $( \ell = 0 , 1 , 2 , \cdots )$ ).因为角动量守但(球对称势)，所以每个分波(用一个特定的 $\ell$ 标记)独立散射，只有相位的变化，而振幅不变.

如果根本不存在势，那么 $\psi _ { 0 } = A \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k z }$ 并且第 $\ell$ 个分波是

$$
\psi_ {0} ^ {(\ell)} = A \mathrm {i} ^ {\ell} (2 \ell + 1) j _ {\ell} (k r) P _ {\ell} (\cos \theta) \qquad (V (r) = 0).
$$

但是由于

$$
j _ {\ell} (x) = \frac {1}{2} \left[ h ^ {(1)} (x) + h _ {l} ^ {(2)} (x) \right] \approx \frac {1}{2 x} \left[ (- \mathrm {i}) ^ {\ell + 1} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} x} + \mathrm {i} ^ {\ell + 1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} x} \right] \quad (x \gg 1).
$$

所以，对于大的

$$
\psi_ {0} ^ {(\ell)} \approx A \frac {2 \ell + 1}{2 \mathrm {i} k r} \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r} - (- 1) ^ {\ell} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k r} \right] P _ {\ell} (\cos \theta) \qquad (V (r) = 0).
$$

其中方括号中的第 项代表一列入射球面波;它来自入射平面波，在引入散射势时它是不改变的.第 项是出射波;它得到了相移 $\delta _ { \ell }$ d由于散射势的存在):

$$
\psi^ {(\ell)} \approx A \frac {2 \ell + 1}{2 \mathrm {i} k r} \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} (k r - 2 \delta \ell)} - (- 1) ^ {\ell} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k r} \right] P _ {\ell} (\cos \theta), \quad (V (r) \neq 0).
$$

分波振幅 $a _ { \ell }$ 与相移 $\delta _ { \ell }$ 的关系:

比较如下两式，

$$
\psi (r, \theta) = A \left\{\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k z} + k \sum_ {\ell = 0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {i} ^ {\ell + 1} (2 \ell + 1) a _ {\ell} h _ {\ell} ^ {(1)} (k r) P _ {\ell} (\cos \theta) \right\}
$$

和 $\psi ^ { ( \ell ) }$ )的第 $\ell$ 个分波在 $r$ 很大时的形式

$$
\psi^ {(r)} \approx A \left\{\frac {2 \ell + 1}{2 \mathrm {i} k r} \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r} - (- 1) ^ {\ell} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k r} \right] + \frac {2 \ell + 1}{r} a _ {\ell} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r} \right\} P _ {\ell} (\cos \theta),
$$

得分波振幅与相移的关系为

$$
a _ {\ell} = \frac {1}{2 \mathrm {i} k} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {2 \mathrm {i} \delta_ {\ell}} - 1\right) = \frac {1}{k} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \delta_ {\ell} \sin (\delta_ {\ell})}.
$$

散射振幅与相移的关系为 $f ( \theta ) = \frac { 1 } { k } \sum _ { \ell = 0 } ^ { \infty } ( 2 \ell + 1 ) \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } \delta _ { \ell } } \sin ( \delta _ { \ell } ) P _ { \ell } ( \cos \theta ) .$

散射界面与相移的关系为 $\sigma = \frac { 4 \pi } { k ^ { 2 } } \sum _ { \ell = 0 } ^ { \infty } \left( 2 \ell + 1 \right) \sin ^ { 2 } ( \delta _ { \ell } )$

# 彼息边的帷事位前

定态薛定诗方程，

$$
- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \nabla^ {2} \psi + V \psi = E \psi ,
$$

可史简洁地写为

$$
(\nabla^ {2} + k ^ {2}) \psi = Q,
$$

其中

$$
k \equiv \frac {\sqrt {2 m E}}{\hbar}, Q \equiv \frac {2 m}{\hbar^ {2}} V \psi .
$$

这在外观上同亥姆霍兹方程的形式一样;这里"非齐次"项 $( Q )$ 本身与 $\psi$ 有关.假设我们可以用函数 $G ( r )$ 来求解具有 $\delta$ 函数"源"的亥姆霍兹方程:

$$
(\nabla^ {2} + k ^ {2}) G (\boldsymbol {r}) = \delta^ {3} (\boldsymbol {r}).
$$

然后可以将 $\psi$ 表示为一个积分

$$
\psi (\boldsymbol {r}) = \int G (\boldsymbol {r} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) Q (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}.
$$

很容易证明它满足薛定诗方程，

$$
\begin{array}{l} (\nabla^ {2} + k ^ {2}) \psi (\boldsymbol {r}) = \int [ (\nabla^ {2} + k ^ {2}) G (\boldsymbol {r} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) ] Q (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} \\ = \int \delta^ {3} (\boldsymbol {r} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) Q (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} = Q (\boldsymbol {r}) \\ \end{array}
$$

$G ( \pmb { r } )$ 称为亥姆霍兹方程的格林函数.

求格林函数，作傅里叶变换将微分方程转化为代数方程，令

$$
G (\pmb {r}) = \frac {1}{(2 \pi) ^ {3 / 2}} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \pmb {s} \cdot \pmb {r}} g (\pmb {s}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \pmb {s}.
$$

则

$$
\left(\nabla^ {2} + k ^ {2}\right) G (\boldsymbol {r}) = \frac {1}{(2 \pi) ^ {3 / 2}} \int \left[ \left(\nabla^ {2} + k ^ {2}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \boldsymbol {s} \cdot \boldsymbol {r}} \right] g (\boldsymbol {s}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {s}.
$$

然而

$$
\nabla^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \boldsymbol {s} \cdot \boldsymbol {r}} = - s ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \boldsymbol {s} \cdot \boldsymbol {r}}, \quad \delta^ {3} (\boldsymbol {r}) = \frac {1}{(2 \pi) ^ {3}} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \boldsymbol {s} \cdot \boldsymbol {r}} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {s}.
$$

因此，方程(" $( \nabla ^ { 2 } + k ^ { 2 } ) G ( \pmb { r } ) = \delta ^ { 3 } ( \pmb { r } )$ 可写为

$$
\frac {1}{(2 \pi) ^ {3 / 2}} \int (- s ^ {2} + k ^ {2}) \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} s \cdot r} g (s) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} s = \frac {1}{(2 \pi) ^ {3}} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} s \cdot r} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} s.
$$

由此，得

$$
g (\boldsymbol {s}) = \frac {1}{(2 \pi) ^ {3 / 2} (k ^ {2} - s ^ {2})}.
$$

将上式代入方程 $G ( \pmb { r } ) = \frac { 1 } { ( 2 \pi ) ^ { 3 / 2 } } \int \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } \pmb { s } \cdot \pmb { r } } g ( \pmb { s } ) \mathrm { d } ^ { 3 } \pmb { s } ,$ 1 一一丁 g(s)d 得到 (ω)3

$$
G (\pmb {r}) = \frac {1}{(2 \pi) ^ {3}} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \pmb {s} \cdot \pmb {r}} \frac {1}{(k ^ {2} - s ^ {2})} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \pmb {s}.
$$

计算 $G ( \pmb { r } ) = \frac { 1 } { ( 2 \pi ) ^ { 3 } } \int \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } \pmb { s } \cdot \pmb { r } } \frac { 1 } { ( k ^ { 2 } - s ^ { 2 } ) } \mathrm { d } ^ { 3 } \pmb { s }$ feis.r 积分，由 $\boldsymbol { s } \cdot \boldsymbol { r } = s r \cos \theta$ 对 $\phi$ 的积分为 $2 \pi$ 对 $\theta$ 的积分为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} s r \cos \theta} \sin \theta \mathrm {d} \theta = - \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} s r \cos \theta}}{\mathrm {i} s r} \Bigg | _ {0} ^ {\pi} = \frac {2 \sin (s r)}{s r}, \\ G (r) = \frac {1}{(2 \pi) ^ {3}} \int \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} s r \cos \theta}}{k ^ {2} - s ^ {2}} \sin (\theta) s ^ {2} \mathrm {d} \theta \mathrm {d} \varphi \mathrm {d} s, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} G \left(r\right) = \frac {1}{4 \pi^ {2} r} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {+ \infty} \frac {s \cdot \sin \left(s r\right)}{k ^ {2} - s ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} s. \\ G (\boldsymbol {r}) = \frac {\mathrm {i}}{8 \pi^ {2} r} \left\{\int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {s \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} s r}}{(s - k) (s + k)} \mathrm {d} s - \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {s \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} s r}}{(s - k) (s + k)} \mathrm {d} s \right\} \\ = \frac {1}{8 \pi^ {2} r} \left(I _ {1} - I _ {2}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

其中，

$$
\begin{array}{l} I _ {1} = \oint \left[ \frac {s \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} s r}}{s + k} \right] \frac {1}{s - k} \mathrm {d} s = 2 \pi \mathrm {i} \left. \left[ \frac {s \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} s r}}{s + k} \right] \right| _ {s = k} = \mathrm {i} \pi \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}, \\ I _ {2} = \oint \left[ \frac {s \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} s r}}{s - k} \right] \frac {1}{s + k} \mathrm {d} s = - 2 \pi \mathrm {i} \left. \left[ \frac {s \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} s r}}{s - k} \right] \right| _ {s = - k} = - \mathrm {i} \pi \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}. \\ \end{array}
$$

亥姆霍兹方程的格林函数为

$$
G (\boldsymbol {r}) = \frac {\mathrm {i}}{8 \pi^ {2} r} \left[ \left(\mathrm {i} \pi \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}\right) - (- \mathrm {i} \pi \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}) \right] = - \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}}{4 \pi r}.
$$

定态薛定诗方程的一般解可以写为

$$
\psi (\boldsymbol {r}) = \psi_ {0} (\boldsymbol {r}) - \frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}} \int \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k | \boldsymbol {r} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} |}}{| \boldsymbol {r} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} |} V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \psi (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {0},
$$

其中 $\psi _ { 0 }$ 满足自由粒子薛定诗方程 $( \nabla ^ { 2 } + k ^ { 2 } ) \psi _ { 0 } = 0$

# -阶玻恩近似:

设势 $V ( r _ { 0 } )$ 在 $r _ { 0 } = 0$ 附近是局域的，需要计算远离散射中心点处的 $\psi ( r )$ . 那么，在$| r | \gg | r _ { 0 } |$ 内所有点对方程

$$
\psi (\boldsymbol {r}) = \psi_ {0} (\boldsymbol {r}) - \frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}} \int \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k | \boldsymbol {r} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} |}}{| \boldsymbol {r} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} |} V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \psi (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}
$$

的积分都有贡献，所以

$$
\left| \boldsymbol {r} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} \right| ^ {2} = r ^ {2} + r _ {0} ^ {2} - 2 \boldsymbol {r} \cdot \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} \approx r ^ {2} \left(1 - 2 \frac {\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}}{r ^ {2}}\right),
$$

因此， $| \pmb { r } - \pmb { r } _ { 0 } | \approx r - \widehat { r } \cdot \pmb { r } _ { 0 }$ $\begin{array} { r } { k \equiv k \widehat { r } } \end{array}$ ，从而 $\mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k | r - r _ { 0 } | } \approx \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k r } \mathrm { e } ^ { - \mathrm { i } k \cdot r _ { 0 } }$ ，因此 $\frac { \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k | r - r _ { 0 } | } } { | r - r _ { 0 } | } \approx$ e $\frac { \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k r } } { r } \mathrm { e } ^ { - \mathrm { i } \boldsymbol { k \cdot r } _ { 0 } }$

在散射情况下，需要用 $\psi _ { 0 } ( \pmb { r } ) = A \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k z }$ 表示入射平面波.当 $r$ 较大时，有

$$
\psi (\boldsymbol {r}) \approx A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k z} - \frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}}{r} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \boldsymbol {k} \cdot \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}} V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \psi (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}.
$$

由此可以读出散射振幅:

$$
f (\theta , \varphi) = - \frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2} A} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \boldsymbol {k} \cdot \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}} V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \psi (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}.
$$

# 玻恩近似:

假设入射平面波在散射区域没有发生实质性变化(其中 $V$ 为非零)，则使用式

$$
\psi (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \approx \psi_ {0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) = A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k z _ {0}} = A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k ^ {\prime} \cdot r _ {0}}
$$

就有意义.其中在积分内 $\boldsymbol { k } ^ { \prime } \equiv k \boldsymbol { \widehat { z } }$

在玻恩近似下有

$$
f (\theta , \phi) = - \frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \left(\boldsymbol {k} ^ {\prime} - \boldsymbol {k}\right) \cdot \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}} V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}.
$$

特别地，对于低能散射，在整个散射区域指数因于基本不变，从而玻恩近似简化为

$$
f (\theta , \phi) = - {\frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}}} \int V (\pmb {r}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \pmb {r}, \quad (\text {低 能 情 形}).
$$

玻恩级数:

坪定诗方程的积分形式为

$$
\psi (\boldsymbol {r}) = \psi_ {0} (\boldsymbol {r}) + \int g (\boldsymbol {r} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \psi (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {0},
$$

其中 $\psi _ { 0 }$ 。为入射波

$$
g (\boldsymbol {r}) \equiv - \frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}}{r}
$$

为格林函数 $V$ 是散射势.用简单的框架表示为

$$
\psi = \psi_ {0} + \int g V \psi .
$$

取此式为 $\psi$ 的表达式，并将其代入积分符号中，选代一次，得

$$
\psi = \psi_ {0} + \int g V \psi_ {0} + \iint g V g V \psi .
$$

重复选代该过程，可得到 $\psi$ 的级数是

$$
\psi = \psi_ {0} + \int g V \psi_ {0} + \iint g V g V \psi_ {0} + \iiint g V g V g V \psi_ {0} + \dots .
$$

在每个被积函数中，只出现入射波函数 $( \psi _ { 0 } )$ )和越来越多的 $g V$ 革次项.

串串串习题 10.1 卢瑟福散射.电荷量为 $q _ { 1 }$ 、动能为 $E$ 的入射粒子被另一电荷量为$q _ { 2 }$ 的静止重粒子散射.

(a) 推导碰撞参量和散射角的关系.①  
(b) 求微分散射截面.   
(c) 证明 卢瑟福散射的总截面无穷大.

解答 (a) 卢瑟福散射是用经典力学求解带电粒子被库仑场散射的问题.散射示意图见图 ，其中 $\theta$ 为散射角.

在散射过程中能 守恒(保守力场)，则(这是个二维问题平面极坐标 1Y)

$$
E = \frac {m}{2} \left(\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\phi} ^ {2}\right) + V (r) = \mathrm {c o n s t}, V (r) \equiv \frac {1}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{r}.
$$

角动量守恒(中心力场)，则

$$
J = m r ^ {2} \dot {\phi} = \mathrm {c o n s t} \Rightarrow \dot {\phi} = \frac {J}{m r ^ {2}}.
$$

![](images/55e9dd9bd129cf31f6cccc93db717abc9b7bff1418b0cadd9e3ceca3e725e25f.jpg)  
10.3

将其代入能量守恒表达式，得

$$
\dot {r} ^ {2} = \frac {2}{m} [ E - V (r) ] - \frac {J ^ {2}}{m ^ {2} r ^ {2}}.
$$

求出 $r$ 作为 $\phi$ 的函数，令 $r = 1 / u$ ，则

$$
\dot {r} = \frac {\mathrm {d} r}{\mathrm {d} t} = \frac {\mathrm {d} r}{\mathrm {d} u} \frac {\mathrm {d} u}{\mathrm {d} \phi} \frac {\mathrm {d} \phi}{\mathrm {d} t} = - \frac {1}{u ^ {2}} \dot {\phi} \frac {\mathrm {d} u}{\mathrm {d} \phi} = - \frac {1}{u ^ {2}} \frac {J}{m r ^ {2}} \frac {\mathrm {d} u}{\mathrm {d} \phi} = - \frac {J}{m} \frac {\mathrm {d} u}{\mathrm {d} \phi}.
$$

因此

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(- \frac {J}{m} \frac {\mathrm {d} u}{\mathrm {d} \phi}\right) ^ {2} = \frac {2}{m} \left(E - \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} u\right) - \frac {J ^ {2}}{m ^ {2}} u ^ {2}, \\ \frac {\mathrm {d} u}{\mathrm {d} \phi} = \sqrt {\frac {2 m}{J ^ {2}} \left(E - \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} u\right) - u ^ {2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

或者

$$
\mathrm {d} \phi = \frac {\mathrm {d} u}{\sqrt {\frac {2 m}{J ^ {2}} \left(E - \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} u\right) - u ^ {2}}}.
$$

$q _ { 1 }$ 粒子从 $r = \infty$ $\overset { \cdot } { u } = 0 \overset { \cdot } { \underset { \cdot } { \mathrm { ~ ~ } } }$ ）， $\phi = 0$ 开始散射，设官离 $q _ { 2 }$ 粒子最近距离为 $r _ { \mathrm { m i n } } \left( u _ { \mathrm { m a x } } \right) , \phi = \phi _ { 0 }$ .显然有

$$
\phi_ {0} = \int_ {0} ^ {u _ {\max }} \frac {\mathrm {d} u}{\sqrt {\frac {2 m}{J ^ {2}} \left(E - \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} u\right) - u ^ {2}}}.
$$

当 $q _ { 1 }$ 粒子以散射角。 $\theta$ 出射时 由对称性

$$
\phi_ {0} + \phi_ {0} + \theta = \pi ,
$$

所以，

$$
\theta = \pi - 2 \phi_ {0} = \pi - 2 \int_ {0} ^ {u _ {\max}} \frac {\mathrm {d} u}{\sqrt {\frac {2 m}{J ^ {2}} \left(E - \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} u\right) - u ^ {2}}}.
$$

设 $u _ { 1 }$ ， $u _ { 2 }$ 是上式分母的两个根

$$
\frac {2 m}{J ^ {2}} \left(E - \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} u\right) - u ^ {2} = (u _ {2} - u) (u - u _ {1}).
$$

由于

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} u}{\mathrm {d} \phi} = \sqrt {\frac {2 m}{J ^ {2}} \left(E - \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} u\right) - u ^ {2}} = \sqrt {(u _ {2} - u) (u - u _ {1})},
$$

所以极大值点 $u _ { \mathrm { m a x } }$ 是一个根，设 $u _ { 2 } > u _ { 1 }$ ，则 $u _ { \mathrm { m a x } } = u _ { 2 }$ ，利用积分公式

$$
\int_ {a} ^ {b} \frac {\mathrm {d} x}{\sqrt {(x - u _ {1}) (u _ {2} - x)}} = - \arcsin \left(\frac {- 2 u + u _ {2} + u _ {1}}{u _ {2} - u _ {1}}\right) \Bigg | _ {a} ^ {b},
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \theta = \pi - 2 \int_ {0} ^ {u _ {\max}} \frac {\mathrm {d} u}{\sqrt {(u _ {\max} - u) (u _ {1} - u)}} \\ = \pi + 2 \arcsin \left(\frac {- 2 u + u _ {\max} + u _ {1}}{u _ {\max} - u _ {1}}\right) \bigg | _ {0} ^ {u _ {\max}} \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \pi + 2 \left[ \arcsin (- 1) - \arcsin \left(\frac {u _ {\max} + u _ {1}}{u _ {\max} - u _ {1}}\right) \right] \\ = \pi + 2 \left[ - \frac {\pi}{2} - \arcsin \left(\frac {u _ {\max } + u _ {1}}{u _ {\max } - u _ {1}}\right) \right] = - 2 \arcsin \left(\frac {u _ {\max } + u _ {1}}{u _ {\max } - u _ {1}}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

现在我们需要解出两个根来，将

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {2 m}{J ^ {2}} \left(E - \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} u\right) - u ^ {2} = 0, \\ u _ {\mathrm {m a x}} = \frac {- \frac {2 m}{J ^ {2}} \frac {q q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} + \sqrt {\left(\frac {2 m}{J ^ {2}} \frac {q q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} + 4 \frac {2 m}{J ^ {2}} E}}{2}, \\ u _ {1} = \frac {- \frac {2 m}{J ^ {2}} \frac {q q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} - \sqrt {\left(\frac {2 m}{J ^ {2}} \frac {q q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} + 4 \frac {2 m}{J ^ {2}} E}}{2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

代入

$E = \frac { 1 } { 2 } m v ^ { 2 } , \quad J = m v b . \quad ( b \ \mathcal { H } \mathbb { H } \backslash \mathbb { E } \backslash \mathbb { H } )$

$$
J ^ {2} = m ^ {2} v ^ {2} b ^ {2} = 2 m E b ^ {2}.
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} u _ {\max } = \frac {- \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{E b ^ {2} 4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} + \sqrt {\left(\frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{E b ^ {2} 4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {4}{b ^ {2}}}}{2} = \frac {A}{2 b ^ {2}} \left[ - 1 + \sqrt {1 + (2 b / A) ^ {2}} \right], \\ u _ {1} = \frac {- \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{E b ^ {2} 4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} - \sqrt {\left(\frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{E b ^ {2} 4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {4}{b ^ {2}}}}{2} = \frac {A}{2 b ^ {2}} \left[ - 1 - \sqrt {1 + (2 b / A) ^ {2}} \right], \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $\begin{array} { r } { A \equiv \frac { q _ { 1 } q _ { 2 } } { E 4 \pi \varepsilon _ { 0 } } } \end{array}$ 旦旦.所以，E命 E:

$$
\frac {u _ {\operatorname* {m a x}} + u _ {1}}{u _ {\operatorname* {m a x}} - u _ {1}} = \frac {- 1}{\sqrt {1 + (2 b / A) ^ {2}}}.
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \theta = - 2 \arcsin \left(\frac {u _ {\operatorname* {m a x}} + u _ {1}}{u _ {\operatorname* {m a x}} - u _ {1}}\right) \to \theta = - 2 \arcsin \left(\frac {- 1}{\sqrt {1 + (2 b / A) ^ {2}}}\right), \\ \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 + (2 b / A) ^ {2}}} = \sin (\theta / 2) \Rightarrow 1 + (2 b / A) ^ {2} = \frac {1}{\sin^ {2} (\theta / 2)}. \\ (2 b / A) ^ {2} = \frac {1}{\sin^ {2} (\theta / 2)} - 1 = \cot^ {2} (\theta / 2) \Rightarrow (2 b / A) = \cot (\theta / 2), \\ b = \frac {A}{2} \cot (\theta / 2) = \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} E} \cot (\theta / 2). \\ \end{array}
$$

(b) 徽分散射截面(方程 (10 均)为

$$
D \left(\theta\right) = \frac {b}{\sin \theta} \left| \frac {\mathrm {d} b}{\mathrm {d} \theta} \right|
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \frac {\frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} E} \cot (\theta / 2)}{2 \sin (\theta / 2) \cos (\theta / 2)} \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} E} \left| \frac {\mathrm {d} \cot (\theta / 2)}{\mathrm {d} \theta} \right| \\ = \left(\frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} E}\right) ^ {2} \frac {1}{2 \sin^ {2} (\theta / 2)} \left| - \frac {1}{2 \sin^ {2} (\theta / 2)} \right| \\ = \left[ \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{1 6 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} E \sin^ {2} (\theta / 2)} \right] ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

(c) 总散射截面为

$$
\sigma = \int D (\theta) \sin \theta \mathrm {d} \theta \mathrm {d} \phi = \left(\frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{1 6 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} E}\right) ^ {2} 2 \pi \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \frac {\sin \theta}{\sin^ {4} (\theta / 2)} \mathrm {d} \theta .
$$

这个积分并不收敛，因为当 $\theta$ 非常接近零时(或者 $\pi$ 时)， sinθ 用。， $\sin \left( \theta / 2 \right) \approx \theta / 2$

$$
\int_ {0} ^ {\varepsilon} \frac {\sin \theta}{\sin^ {4} (\theta / 2)} \mathrm {d} \theta = 1 6 \int_ {0} ^ {\varepsilon} \frac {1}{\theta^ {3}} \mathrm {d} \theta = 8 \left(\left. \frac {- 1}{\theta^ {2}} \right| _ {0} ^ {\varepsilon}\right)\rightarrow \infty .
$$

# 习题 10.2 针对一维和二维散射，构造与方程 (10.12) 相对应的表达式.

解答对于一维形式，由于散射波只能向前 $\theta = 0$ 透射波)或向后 $\theta = \pi$ 反射波)传播，而且波幅不会衰减.因此(设散射中心位于 $z = 0$ ）

![](images/4f136111d6b62a1ec3bcd3658647438e1641d28d3cc5dd3bcc687dd5761b1244.jpg)  
10 .4

$$
\psi \left(z\right) = A \left\{\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k z} + f \left[ \left(1 - z / | z |\right) \pi / 2 \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k z} \right\},
$$

其中当 $z > 0$ 时， $f \left[ \left( 1 - z / | z | \right) \pi / 2 \right] = f \left( 0 \right)$ ，散射波向前传播;当$z < 0$ 时， $f \left[ \left( 1 - z / | z | \right) \pi / 2 \right] = f \left( \pi \right)$ ，散射披向后传播.

对于二维形式，以靶粒子为困心建立极坐标，如固 10.4 所示-此时考虑到散射披概率密度对距离靶粒子 $r$ 处的半径为 $r$ 的困积分有限，可仿照三维形式(方程 (10.12)) 写出其二维形式

$$
\psi (r, \theta) = A \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k z} + f (\theta) \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}}{\sqrt {r}} \right],
$$

其中。 $\theta$ 为极角.

习题 从方程 开始，证明方程 提示 利用勒让德多项式的正交性，证明:具有不同 $\ell$ 值的系数必须分别为零.

证明方程 (10.32) 两边同时乘以 $P _ { \ell ^ { \prime } }$ (cos8) 并对 $\theta$ 积分，得

$$
\sum_ {\ell = 0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {i} ^ {\ell} (2 \ell + 1) \left[ j _ {\ell} (k r) + \mathrm {i} k a _ {\ell} h _ {\ell} ^ {(1)} (k r) \right] \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} P _ {\ell} (\cos \theta) P _ {\ell^ {\prime}} (\cos \theta) \sin \theta \mathrm {d} \theta = 0.
$$

而积分

$$
\int_ {0} ^ {\pi} P _ {\ell} (\cos \theta) P _ {\ell^ {\prime}} (\cos \theta) \sin \theta d \theta = \frac {2 \delta_ {\ell \ell^ {\prime}}}{2 \ell + 1},
$$

所以

$$
\sum_ {\ell = 0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {i} ^ {\ell} (2 \ell + 1) \left[ j _ {\ell} (k r) + \mathrm {i} k a _ {\ell} h _ {\ell} ^ {(1)} (k r) \right] \frac {2 \delta_ {\ell \ell^ {\prime}}}{2 \ell + 1} = 0,
$$

$$
\mathrm {i} ^ {\ell} (2 \ell + 1) \left[ j _ {\ell} (k r) + \mathrm {i} k a _ {\ell} h _ {\ell} ^ {(1)} (k r) \right] \frac {2}{2 \ell + 1} = 0, (\ell^ {\prime} \rightarrow \ell)
$$

$$
a _ {\ell} = \frac {\mathrm {i} j _ {\ell} (k r)}{k h _ {\ell} ^ {(1)} (k r)}.
$$

串串习题 10.4 考虑球面 $\delta$ 函数球壳低能散射:

$$
V (r) = \alpha \delta (r - a),
$$

其中 $\alpha$ 和 $a$ 是正的常数，计算散射振幅 $f \left( \theta \right)$ 、微分截面 $D \left( \theta \right)$ 以及总截面 $\sigma$ ，假定$k a \ll 1$ ，从而仅有 $\ell = 0$ 的项起主要作用. (为简化问题，从一开始我们就舍去所有的 $\ell \neq 0$ 项.)当然，主要问题是确定 $C _ { 0 }$ 答案用无量纲量 $\beta \equiv 2 m a \alpha / \hbar ^ { 2 }$ 来表示，

解答只考虑 $\langle \ell = 0 \rangle$ ，由方程 (10.29) ，在球壳外部区域，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \psi = A \left[ j _ {0} (k r) + \mathrm {i} k a _ {0} h _ {0} ^ {(1)} (k r) \right] P _ {0} (\cos \theta) = A \left[ \frac {\sin (k r)}{k r} + \mathrm {i} k a _ {0} \left(- \mathrm {i} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}}{k r}\right) \right] \\ = A \left[ \frac {\sin (k r)}{k r} + a _ {0} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}}{r} \right], \quad r > a \\ \end{array}
$$

在内部区域，由方程 (10.18) $\dot { \boldsymbol { n } } \varrho$ 在原点处发散)，得

$$
\psi = \left[ B j _ {0} (k r) + C n _ {0} (k r) \right] = B \frac {\sin (k r)}{k r}, \quad r <   a.
$$

(因为 $r  0 , n _ { 0 }  \infty$ ，所以必须有 $C = 0$ . 波函数在 $r = a$ 处连续，故

$$
A \left[ \frac {\sin (k a)}{k a} + a _ {0} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a}}{a} \right] = B \frac {\sin (k a)}{k a}.
$$

在 $r = a$ 处，波函数的导数有跃变 (15 函数势)，积分薛定诗方程 $\langle \ell = 0 \rangle$ )

$$
\lim  _ {\varepsilon \rightarrow 0} \int_ {a - \varepsilon} ^ {a + \varepsilon} \left[ \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} u}{\mathrm {d} r ^ {2}} + k ^ {2} u - \frac {2 m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} \delta (r - a) u \right] = 0,
$$

$$
\lim _ {\varepsilon \to 0} \frac {\mathrm {d} u}{\mathrm {d} r} \bigg | _ {a - \varepsilon} ^ {a + \varepsilon} = \frac {2 m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} u (a) \to \Delta \left(\frac {\mathrm {d} u}{\mathrm {d} r}\right) = \frac {2 m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} u (a).
$$

由于 $\psi = u ( r ) / r$ 所以

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} u}{\mathrm {d} r} = r \frac {\mathrm {d} \psi}{\mathrm {d} r} + \psi \rightarrow \Delta \left(\frac {\mathrm {d} u}{\mathrm {d} r}\right) = a \Delta \left(\frac {\mathrm {d} \psi}{\mathrm {d} r}\right).
$$

所以

$$
\Delta \left(\frac {\mathrm {d} \psi}{\mathrm {d} r}\right) = \frac {2 m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} a u (a) = \frac {\beta}{a} \psi (a),
$$

llP

$$
\begin{array}{l} A \left[ \frac {k \cos (k a)}{k a} - \frac {\sin (k a)}{k a ^ {2}} + a _ {0} \frac {\mathrm {i} k \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a}}{a} - a _ {0} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a}}{a ^ {2}} \right] - B \left[ \frac {k \cos (k a)}{k a} - \frac {\sin (k a)}{k a ^ {2}} \right] \\ = \frac {\beta}{a} B \frac {\sin (k a)}{k a}. \\ \end{array}
$$

由披函数连续的条件，上式化简为

$$
A \left[ \frac {k \cos (k a)}{k a} + a _ {0} \frac {\mathrm {i} k \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a}}{a} \right] - B \left[ \frac {k \cos (k a)}{k a} \right] = \frac {\beta}{a} B \frac {\sin (k a)}{k a},
$$

$$
A \left[ \cos (k a) + a _ {0} \mathrm {i} k \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a} \right] = B \sin (k a) \left[ \cot (k a) + \frac {\beta}{k a} \right].
$$

再次利用披函数连续条件捎去 $B$ ，得

$$
A \left[ \frac {\sin (k a)}{k a} + a _ {0} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a}}{a} \right] = B \frac {\sin (k a)}{k a},
$$

$$
A \left[ \cos (k a) + a _ {0} \mathrm {i} k \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a} \right] = A \left[ \cot (k a) + \frac {\beta}{k a} \right] \left[ \sin (k a) + a _ {0} k \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a} \right],
$$

$$
\cos (k a) + a _ {0} \mathrm {i} k \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a} = \left[ \cot (k a) + \frac {\beta}{k a} \right] \left[ \sin (k a) + a _ {0} k \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a} \right],
$$

$$
\cos (k a) + a _ {0} \mathrm {i} k \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a} = \cos (k a) + \frac {\beta}{k a} \sin (k a) + a _ {0} k \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a} \cot (k a) + \frac {\beta}{k a} a _ {0} k \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a},
$$

$$
a _ {0} \mathrm {i} k \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a} = \left[ \frac {\beta}{k a} \sin (k a) + a _ {0} k \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a} \cot (k a) + \frac {\beta}{k a} a _ {0} k \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a} \right],
$$

$$
\mathrm {i} k a _ {0} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a} \left[ 1 + \mathrm {i} \cot (k a) + \mathrm {i} \frac {\beta}{k a} \right] = \frac {\beta}{k a} \sin (k a).
$$

由 $k a \ll 1 \to \sin \left( k a \right) \approx k a , \cos \left( k a \right) \approx 1 .$ $\cos { ( k a ) } \approx 1$ 土式为

$$
\mathrm {i} k a _ {0} (1 + \mathrm {i} k a) \left(1 + \mathrm {i} \frac {1}{k a} + \mathrm {i} \frac {\beta}{k a}\right) = \frac {\beta}{k a} k a,
$$

$$
\mathrm {i} k a _ {0} (1 + \mathrm {i} k a) \left(1 + \mathrm {i} \frac {1}{k a} + \mathrm {i} \frac {\beta}{k a}\right) = \frac {\beta}{k a} k a,
$$

$$
\mathrm {i} k a _ {0} \left[ 1 + \frac {\mathrm {i}}{k a} (1 + \beta) + \mathrm {i} k a - 1 - \beta \right] \approx \mathrm {i} k a _ {0} \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{k a} (1 + \beta) \right] = \beta ,
$$

$$
a _ {0} = \frac {a \beta}{1 + \beta}.
$$

由方程 (10.25) $f \left( \theta \right) = \sum _ { \ell = 0 } ^ { \infty } \left( 2 \ell + 1 \right) a _ { \ell } P _ { \ell } \left( \cos \theta \right)$ ，所以只考虑 $\ell = 0$ 分波时，散射振帽、微分散射截面、散射截面分别为

$$
f (\theta) \approx a _ {0} = - \frac {\beta a}{1 + \beta},
$$

$$
D (\theta) = | f (\theta) | ^ {2} \approx \frac {\beta^ {2} a ^ {2}}{(1 + \beta) ^ {2}},
$$

$$
\sigma = \int D (\theta) \mathrm {d} \Omega = 4 \pi \frac {\beta^ {2} a ^ {2}}{(1 + \beta) ^ {2}}.
$$

习题 10.5 质量为 $m$ ，能量为 $E$ 的粒子从左边入射到如下势上:

$$
V \left(x\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} 0, & \left(x <   - a\right), \\ - V _ {0}, & \left(- a \leqslant x \leqslant 0\right), \\ \infty , & \left(x > 0\right). \end{array} \right.
$$

(叫设入射波是 $\boldsymbol { A } \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k x }$ 其中 $k = { \sqrt { 2 m E } } { \Big / } \hbar )$ ，求反射波  
验证反射波振幅与入射波振幅相同.

(c) 对于一个非常深的势阱 $( E \ll V _ { 0 } )$ )，求相移 $\delta$ (方程 (10.40)).

解答 (a) 如固 10.5 所示，将 $x \leqslant 0$ 区域分为 区， 区中 $V \left( x \right) = - V _ { 0 }$ ( $- a \leqslant x \leqslant 0 )$ II中 $V \left( x \right) = 0 \left( x \leqslant - a \right)$ . 定态薛定诲方程的解为

I区:

$$
\psi_ {1} = A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} + B \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x},
$$

其中 $x < - a , k \equiv { \frac { \sqrt { 2 m E } } { \hbar } } , A \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k x }$ 为入射波 $\boldsymbol { B } \mathrm { e } ^ { - \mathrm { i } k \boldsymbol { x } }$ 为反射波-

E 区:

$$
\psi_ {2} = C \sin \left(k ^ {\prime} x\right) + D \cos \left(k ^ {\prime} x\right),
$$

其中 -a < x < k' 42mT 问) $- a < x < 0 , k ^ { \prime } \equiv \frac { \sqrt { 2 m \left( E + V _ { 0 } \right) } } { \hbar } .$ h

在 $x = 0$ 处， $\psi = 0$ ，因此 $D = 0$ ；在 $x = - a$ 处，由 $\psi$ 及其导数连续条件，得

![](images/698d24cb15ec8767ff69262340be5850ebf817ada7fc37336d57115afaf68ecf.jpg)  
10.5

$$
A \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k a} + B \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a} = - C \sin \left(k ^ {\prime} a\right),
$$

$$
\mathrm {i} k \left(A \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k a} - B \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a}\right) = k ^ {\prime} C \cos \left(k ^ {\prime} a\right),
$$

两式相除消去 $C$ ，得

$$
\frac {\mathrm {i} k \left(A \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k a} - B \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a}\right)}{A \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k a} + B \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k a}} = - k ^ {\prime} \cot (k ^ {\prime} a),
$$

求得的 $B$ 与 $A$ 的关系为

$$
\mathrm {i} k \left(A \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k a} - B \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a}\right) = - k ^ {\prime} \cot k ^ {\prime} a \left(A \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k a} + B \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a}\right),
$$

$$
\left(k ^ {\prime} \cot \left(k ^ {\prime} a\right) - \mathrm {i} k\right) B \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a} = - \left(k ^ {\prime} \cot k ^ {\prime} a + \mathrm {i} k\right) A \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k a},
$$

$$
B = - \frac {k ^ {\prime} \cot (k ^ {\prime} a) + \mathrm {i} k}{k ^ {\prime} \cot (k ^ {\prime} a) - \mathrm {i} k} A \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} k a} = A \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} k a} \frac {k - \mathrm {i} k ^ {\prime} \cot (k ^ {\prime} a)}{k + \mathrm {i} k ^ {\prime} \cot (k ^ {\prime} a)}.
$$

故所求反射披为

$$
B \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x} = A \frac {k - \mathrm {i} k ^ {\prime} \cot \left(k ^ {\prime} a\right)}{k + \mathrm {i} k ^ {\prime} \cot \left(k ^ {\prime} a\right)} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} k a} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x}.
$$

(b)

$$
| B | ^ {2} = | A | ^ {2} \left| \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} k a} \frac {k - \mathrm {i} k ^ {\prime} \cot (k ^ {\prime} a)}{k + \mathrm {i} k ^ {\prime} \cot (k ^ {\prime} a)} \right| ^ {2} = | A | ^ {2},
$$

或者

$$
B = A \frac {k - \mathrm {i} k ^ {\prime} \cot \left(k ^ {\prime} a\right)}{k + \mathrm {i} k ^ {\prime} \cot \left(k ^ {\prime} a\right)} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} k a} = A \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} k a} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \theta},
$$

其中

$$
\tan \theta = \frac {k ^ {\prime} \cot \left(k ^ {\prime} a\right)}{k}.
$$

反射波与入射披相比只多了一个固定的相位，它们的模平方一样.

(c) $x < - a$ 区域的波函数为

$$
\psi (x) = A \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} + \frac {k - \mathrm {i} k ^ {\prime} \cot \left(k ^ {\prime} a\right)}{k + \mathrm {i} k ^ {\prime} \cot \left(k ^ {\prime} a\right)} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} k a} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x} \right].
$$

但是该区域的披函数形式应为(方程(口10.4钊0))

$$
\psi (x) = A \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} - \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} (2 \delta - k x)} \right], \quad (V (x) \neq 0).
$$

所以

$$
\frac {k - \mathrm {i} k ^ {\prime} \cot \left(k ^ {\prime} a\right)}{k + \mathrm {i} k ^ {\prime} \cot \left(k ^ {\prime} a\right)} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} k a} = - \mathrm {e} ^ {2 \mathrm {i} \delta},
$$

因为 $E \ll V _ { 0 } \to k \ll k ^ { \prime }$ 所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {- \mathrm {i} k ^ {\prime} \cot \left(k ^ {\prime} a\right)}{+ \mathrm {i} k ^ {\prime} \cot \left(k ^ {\prime} a\right)} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} k a} = - \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} k a} = - \mathrm {e} ^ {2 \mathrm {i} \delta} \\ \Rightarrow \delta = - k a. \\ \end{array}
$$

# 硬球散射(例题 10码的分波相移 习题 10.6 $\left( \delta _ { \ell } \right)$ 是多少?

对于硬球散射，分被振幅为(方程 (10.33))解答

$$
a _ {\ell} = \frac {\mathrm {i} j _ {\ell} (k a)}{k h _ {\ell} ^ {(1)} (k a)} = \frac {\mathrm {i} j _ {\ell} (k a)}{k [ j _ {\ell} (k a) + \mathrm {i} n _ {\ell} (k a) ]}.
$$

而由方程 (10 .46)

$$
a _ {\ell} = \frac {1}{2 i k} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {2 \mathrm {i} \delta_ {\ell}} - 1\right) = \frac {1}{k} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \delta_ {\ell}} \sin \left(\delta_ {\ell}\right),
$$

对比可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \delta} \sin (\delta_ {\ell}) = \frac {\mathrm {i} j _ {\ell} (k a)}{\left[ j _ {\ell} (k a) + \mathrm {i} n _ {\ell} (k a) \right]} \\ = \mathrm {i} \frac {1}{1 + \mathrm {i} n _ {\ell} / j _ {\ell}} \\ = \mathrm {i} \frac {1 - \mathrm {i} n _ {\ell} / j _ {\ell}}{1 + (n _ {\ell} / j _ {\ell}) ^ {2}} \\ = \frac {n _ {\ell} / j _ {\ell} + \mathrm {i}}{1 + \left(n _ {\ell} / j _ {\ell}\right) ^ {2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

两边实部和虚部应分别相等，所以

$$
\cos \delta_ {\ell} \sin \delta_ {\ell} = \frac {n _ {\ell} / j _ {\ell}}{1 + (n _ {\ell} / j _ {\ell}) ^ {2}}, \quad \sin^ {2} \delta_ {\ell} = \frac {1}{1 + (n _ {\ell} / j _ {\ell}) ^ {2}}.
$$

两式相除，得

$$
\tan \delta_ {\ell} = \frac {1}{n _ {\ell} / j _ {\ell}}, \quad \delta_ {\ell} = \arctan \left[ \frac {j _ {\ell} (k a)}{n _ {\ell} (k a)} \right].
$$

习题 10.7 $\delta$ 函数壳势散射 $( \ell = 0 )$ ) 的分波相移 $\delta _ { 0 } \left( k \right)$ (习题 10.4).假设当$r  0$ 时，径向波函数 $u \left( r \right)$ 趋于 答案:

$$
- \operatorname {a r c c o t} \left[ \cot \left(k a\right) + {\frac {k a}{\beta \sin^ {2} \left(k a\right)}} \right], \text {其 中} \beta \equiv {\frac {2 m \alpha a}{\hbar^ {2}}}.
$$

解答 $r < a$ 区域，势能为零， $\langle \ell = 0 \rangle$ 的径向披函数 $u \left( r \right) = r R \left( r \right)$ 满足方程

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} u}{\mathrm {d} r ^ {2}} + k ^ {2} u = 0, \quad r <   a, \quad k \equiv \frac {\sqrt {2 m E}}{\hbar}.
$$

方程解为

$$
u (r) = A \sin \left(k r + \delta_ {0} ^ {\prime}\right).
$$

$R \left( r \right) = \frac { u \left( r \right) } { r }$ $r  0$ 时有限 $\delta _ { 0 } ^ { \prime } = 0$ ，所以 $\begin{array} { r } { u \left( r \right) = A \sin \left( k r \right) , r < a . } \end{array}$

在 $r > a$ 区域，势能也为零，径向被函数 $u \left( r \right)$ 为

$$
u (r) = C \sin \left(k r + \delta_ {0}\right), \quad (r > a).
$$

由被函数在 $r = a$ 处连续，得

$$
C [ \sin (k a + \delta_ {0}) ] = A \sin (k a)
$$

及导数跃变条件 ( $\delta$ 函数产生的跃变)

$$
\left. \frac {\mathrm {d} u}{\mathrm {d} r} \right| _ {a + 0 ^ {+}} - \left. \frac {\mathrm {d} u}{\mathrm {d} r} \right| _ {a + 0 ^ {-}} = \frac {2 m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} u (a),
$$

得

$$
C k \cos (k a + \delta_ {0}) - A k \cos (k a) = \frac {2 m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} A \sin (k a),
$$

$$
C \cos (k a + \delta_ {0}) = A \left[ \cos (k a) + \frac {2 m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2} k} \sin (k a) \right].
$$

将其与 $C \left[ \sin \left( k a + \delta _ { 0 } \right) \right] = A \sin \left( k a \right)$ 相除，得

$$
\cot \left(k a + \delta_ {0}\right) = \cot \left(k a\right) + \frac {2 m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2} k},
$$

$$
\frac {\cos \delta_ {0} \cos (k a) - \sin \delta_ {0} \sin (k a)}{\sin \delta_ {0} \cos (k a) + \sin (k a) \cos \delta_ {0}} = \frac {\cos (k a)}{\sin (k a)} + \frac {\beta}{k a}, \quad \beta \equiv \frac {2 m \alpha a}{\hbar^ {2}}
$$

$$
\cos \delta_ {0} \cos (k a) - \sin \delta_ {0} \sin (k a) = (\sin \delta_ {0} \cos (k a) + \sin (k a) \cos \delta_ {0}) \left(\frac {\cos (k a)}{\sin (k a)} + \frac {\beta}{k a}\right),
$$

$$
\cos \delta_ {0} \sin (k a) \frac {\beta}{k a} = - \sin \delta_ {0} \sin (k a) - \sin \delta_ {0} \cos (k a) \left(\frac {\cos (k a)}{\sin (k a)} + \frac {\beta}{k a}\right),
$$

$$
\cos \delta_ {0} \sin (k a) \frac {\beta}{k a} = - \sin \delta_ {0} \left(\sin (k a) + \frac {\cos^ {2} (k a)}{\sin (k a)} + \cos (k a) \frac {\beta}{k a}\right),
$$

$$
\cos \delta_ {0} \sin (k a) \frac {\beta}{k a} = - \sin \delta_ {0} \left(\frac {1}{\sin (k a)} + \cos (k a) \frac {\beta}{k a}\right),
$$

$$
\cot \delta_ {0} = - \left(\cot (k a) + \frac {k a}{\beta \sin^ {2} (k a)}\right).
$$

所以

$$
\delta_ {0} = - \operatorname {a r c c o t} \left[ \cot (k a) + \frac {k a}{\beta \sin^ {2} (k a)} \right].
$$

当 $k a \ll 1$ 时 $\mathrm { ~  ~ { ~ \delta _ { 0 } ~ } ~ }$ 不一定很小)，取近似 $\sin ( k a ) \approx k a$ $\cos ( k a ) \approx 1$ ，得

$$
\frac {\cos \delta_ {0} - k a \sin \delta_ {0}}{\sin \delta_ {0} + k a \cos \delta_ {0}} \approx \frac {1}{k a} + \frac {\beta}{k a} = \frac {1 + \beta}{k a},
$$

$$
k a \left(\cos \delta_ {0} - k a \sin \delta_ {0}\right) = (1 + \beta) (\sin \delta_ {0} + k a \cos \delta_ {0}),
$$

$$
- (k a) ^ {2} \sin \delta_ {0} = (1 + \beta) \sin \delta_ {0} + \beta k a \cos \delta_ {0},
$$

$$
\left[ 1 + \beta + (k a) ^ {2} \right] \sin \delta_ {0} = \beta k a \cos \delta_ {0},
$$

$$
\left[ 1 + \beta + (k a) ^ {2} \right] ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \delta_ {0} = (\beta k a) ^ {2} \left(1 - \sin^ {2} \delta_ {0}\right),
$$

$$
\sin^ {2} \delta_ {0} = \frac {(\beta k a) ^ {2}}{\left[ 1 + \beta + (k a) ^ {2} + (\beta k a) ^ {2} \right] ^ {2}} \approx \frac {(\beta k a) ^ {2}}{(1 + \beta) ^ {2}}.
$$

所以

$$
D (\theta) = | f (\theta) | ^ {2} = \frac {1}{k ^ {2}} \sin^ {2} \delta_ {0} \approx \left(\frac {\beta a}{1 + \beta}\right) ^ {2}.
$$

这与习题 10.4中结果一样.

习题 10.8 通过直接代入方法，验证方程 (10.65) 满足方程 (10.52). 提示 $\nabla ^ { 2 } \left( 1 / r \right) =$ 4πð3 (r).①

解答

$$
\nabla G \left(\boldsymbol {r}\right) = \nabla \left(- \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}}{4 \pi r}\right) = \left[ - \frac {\mathrm {i} k}{r} \nabla r - \nabla \left(\frac {1}{r}\right) \right] \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}}{4 \pi},
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \nabla \cdot \nabla G (\boldsymbol {r}) = \left[ - \mathrm {i} k \nabla \left(\frac {1}{r}\right) \nabla r - \mathrm {i} k \frac {1}{r} \nabla^ {2} r - \nabla^ {2} \left(\frac {1}{r}\right) \right] \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}}{4 \pi} \\ + \left[ - \frac {\mathrm {i} k}{r} \nabla r - \nabla \left(\frac {1}{r}\right) \right] \mathrm {i} k \nabla r \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}}{4 \pi} \\ = \left[ - 2 \mathrm {i} k \nabla \left(\frac {1}{r}\right) \nabla r - \mathrm {i} k \frac {1}{r} \nabla^ {2} r + k ^ {2} \frac {1}{r} (\nabla r) ^ {2} - \nabla^ {2} \left(\frac {1}{r}\right) \right] \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}}{4 \pi}. \\ \end{array}
$$

在上式中代入

$$
\nabla r = \frac {\boldsymbol {r}}{r}, \quad \nabla \left(\frac {1}{r}\right) = - \frac {\boldsymbol {r}}{r ^ {3}}, \quad \nabla^ {2} (r) = \frac {2}{r}, \quad \nabla^ {2} (1 / r) = - 4 \pi \delta^ {3} (\boldsymbol {r}),
$$

得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \nabla \cdot \nabla G (\boldsymbol {r}) = \left[ 2 \mathrm {i} k \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} - \mathrm {i} k \frac {2}{r ^ {2}} + k ^ {2} \frac {1}{r} + 4 \pi \delta^ {3} (\boldsymbol {r}) \right] \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}}{4 \pi} \\ = \left[ k ^ {2} \frac {1}{r} + 4 \pi \delta^ {3} (r) \right] \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}}{4 \pi} \\ = - k ^ {2} G (\boldsymbol {r}) + \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r} \delta^ {3} (\boldsymbol {r}). \\ \end{array}
$$

所以，

$$
\left(\nabla^ {2} + k ^ {2}\right) G (\boldsymbol {r}) = \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r} \delta^ {3} (\boldsymbol {r}).
$$

而 $\mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k r } \delta ^ { 3 } \left( r \right)$ 等同于 $\delta ^ { 3 } \left( r \right)$ 因为对于任何函数 $f \left( x \right)$ 有

$$
\int_ {- \varepsilon} ^ {\varepsilon} f (x) \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r} \delta^ {3} (\boldsymbol {r}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} = f (0),
$$

所以，

$$
\left(\nabla^ {2} + k ^ {2}\right) G \left(\boldsymbol {r}\right) = \delta^ {3} \left(\boldsymbol {r}\right).
$$

串串习题 10.9 对于适当的 $V$ 和 $E$ 证明:氢原子基态(方程 (4.80)) 满足积分形式薛定诲方程(注意 $E$ 为负值，所以 $k = \mathrm { i } \kappa$ ，其中 $\kappa \equiv \sqrt { - 2 m E } / \hbar )$ .

解答氢原子基础函数 $\psi \left( r \right) = { \frac { 1 } { \sqrt { \pi a ^ { 3 } } } } \mathrm { e } ^ { - { \frac { r } { a } } }$ ，其中 $a \equiv \frac { 4 \pi \varepsilon _ { 0 } \hbar ^ { 2 } } { m e ^ { 2 } }$ 为玻尔半径vπ 13

相应的势场为

$$
V (r) = - \frac {1}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {e ^ {2}}{r} = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{m a} \frac {1}{r}.
$$

在氢原子的情况下没有入射披函数，因而 $\psi _ { 0 } \left( \pmb { r } \right) = 0$ . 于是我们要证明的是下面的式子:

$$
- \frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}} \int \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k | \boldsymbol {r} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} |}}{| \boldsymbol {r} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} |} V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \psi (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} = \psi (\boldsymbol {r}).
$$

通过化简左边部分来证明它等于右边部分.代入被函数和势能，

左边 $= - \frac { m } { 2 \pi \hbar ^ { 2 } } \left( - \frac { \hbar ^ { 2 } } { m a } \right) \frac { 1 } { \sqrt { \pi a ^ { 3 } } } \int \frac { \mathrm { e } ^ { - | r - r _ { 0 } | / a } } { | r - r _ { 0 } | } \frac { 1 } { r _ { 0 } } \mathrm { e } ^ { - r _ { 0 } / a } \mathrm { d } ^ { 3 } r _ { 0 }$ e一俨旷内/

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \frac {1}{2 \pi a} \frac {1}{\sqrt {\pi a ^ {3}}} \int \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- \sqrt {r ^ {2} + r _ {0} ^ {2} - 2 r r _ {0} \cos \theta} / a}}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} + r _ {0} ^ {2} - 2 r r _ {0} \cos \theta}} \frac {1}{r _ {0}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- r _ {0} / a} r _ {0} ^ {2} 2 \pi \sin \theta \mathrm {d} \theta \mathrm {d} r _ {0} \\ = \frac {1}{a \sqrt {\pi a ^ {3}}} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {d} r _ {0} \mathrm {e} ^ {- r _ {0} / a} r _ {0} \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- \sqrt {r ^ {2} + r _ {0} ^ {2} - 2 r r _ {0} \cos \theta} / a}}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} + r _ {0} ^ {2} - 2 r r _ {0} \cos \theta}} \sin \theta \mathrm {d} \theta \\ = \frac {1}{a \sqrt {\pi a ^ {3}}} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {- r _ {0} / a} r _ {0} \mathrm {d} r _ {0} \left(- \frac {a}{r r _ {0}}\right) \left(\left. \mathrm {e} ^ {- \sqrt {r ^ {2} + r _ {0} ^ {2} - 2 r r _ {0} \cos \theta} / a} \right| _ {0} ^ {\pi}\right) \\ = - \frac {1}{r \sqrt {\pi a ^ {3}}} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {d} r _ {0} \mathrm {e} ^ {- r _ {0} / a} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {- (r + r _ {0}) / a} - \mathrm {e} ^ {- | r - r _ {0} | / a}\right) \\ = - \frac {1}{r} \frac {1}{\sqrt {\pi a ^ {3}}} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {- r / a} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 r _ {0} / a} \mathrm {d} r _ {0} - \mathrm {e} ^ {- r / a} \int_ {0} ^ {r} \mathrm {d} r _ {0} - \mathrm {e} ^ {r / a} \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 r _ {0} / a} \mathrm {d} r _ {0}\right) \\ = - \frac {1}{r} \frac {1}{\sqrt {\pi a ^ {3}}} \left(\frac {a}{2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- r / a} - r \mathrm {e} ^ {- r / a} - \frac {a}{2} \mathrm {e} ^ {r / a} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 r / a}\right) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {\pi a ^ {3}}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- r / a}. \\ \end{array}
$$

右边正甜甜披函数，所以时瞰(叫..J $( \mathrm { e } ^ { - r / a } / \sqrt { \pi a ^ { 3 } } )$ 满足薛定时程的积分形式

事习题 10.10 在玻恩近似下，求任意能量的软球散射的散射振幅，并证明所得结果在低能极限情况下变为方程 (10.82).

解答在玻恩近似下，散射振幅的表达式(方程 (10.79))

$$
f \left(\theta , \phi\right) \approx - \frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \left(\boldsymbol {k} ^ {\prime} - \boldsymbol {k}\right) \cdot \boldsymbol {r _ {0}}} V \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}\right) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}.
$$

令 $\kappa = k ^ { \prime } - k$ 并让极轴 $\left( z _ { 0 } \right)$ 方向沿 $\boldsymbol { \kappa }$ 方向，所以 $( { \pmb k } ^ { \prime } - { \pmb k } ) \cdot { \pmb r } _ { 0 } = { \pmb \kappa } \cdot { \pmb r } _ { 0 } = \kappa r \cos { \theta _ { 0 } }$ ，其中 $\theta _ { 0 }$ 是 $\boldsymbol { \kappa }$ 与$\mathbf { \boldsymbol { r } } _ { 0 }$ 的夹角，所以

$$
f (\theta , \phi) = - \frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \kappa r _ {0} \cos \theta_ {0}} V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) r _ {0} ^ {2} \sin \theta_ {0} \mathrm {d} r _ {0} \mathrm {d} \theta_ {0} \mathrm {d} \phi_ {0}.
$$

对于软球势，

$$
V \left(r\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} V _ {0}, & r \leqslant a, \\ 0, & r > a. \end{array} \right.
$$

对 $\theta _ { 0 }$ 和 $\phi _ { 0 }$ 进行积分，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} f (\theta , \phi) = - \frac {m V _ {0}}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}} \int_ {0} ^ {a} r _ {0} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {0} \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \kappa r _ {0} \cos \theta_ {0}} \sin \theta_ {0} \mathrm {d} \theta_ {0} \int_ {0} ^ {2 \pi} \mathrm {d} \phi_ {0} \\ = - \frac {m V _ {0}}{\hbar^ {2}} \int_ {0} ^ {a} r _ {0} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {0} \left(\left. \frac {1}{\mathrm {i} \kappa r _ {0}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \kappa r _ {0} \cos \theta_ {0}} \right| _ {0} ^ {\pi}\right) \\ = - \frac {m V _ {0}}{\hbar^ {2}} \int_ {0} ^ {a} r _ {0} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {0} \left(\frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \kappa r _ {0}} - \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \kappa r _ {0}}}{\mathrm {i} \kappa r _ {0}}\right) \\ = - \frac {2 m V _ {0}}{\kappa \hbar^ {2}} \int_ {0} ^ {a} r _ {0} \sin (\kappa r _ {0}) \mathrm {d} r _ {0} \\ = - \frac {2 m V _ {0}}{\kappa \hbar^ {2}} \left[ \frac {1}{\kappa^ {2}} \sin (\kappa r _ {0}) - \frac {r _ {0}}{\kappa} \cos (\kappa r _ {0}) \right] \Bigg | _ {0} ^ {a} \\ = - \frac {2 m V _ {0}}{\kappa \hbar^ {2}} \left[ \frac {1}{\kappa^ {2}} \sin (\kappa a) - \frac {a}{\kappa} \cos (\kappa a) \right] \\ = - \frac {2 m V _ {0}}{\kappa^ {3} \hbar^ {2}} \left[ \sin (\kappa a) - \kappa a \cos (\kappa a) \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

由 $\kappa = 2 k \sin ( \theta / 2 ) , k a \ll 1  \kappa a \ll 1$ ，所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} f (\theta , \phi) = - \frac {2 m V _ {0}}{\kappa^ {3} \hbar^ {2}} [ \sin (\kappa a) - \kappa a \cos (\kappa a) ] \\ \approx - \frac {2 m V _ {0}}{\kappa^ {3} \hbar^ {2}} \left\{\left[ \kappa a - \frac {1}{3 !} (\kappa a) ^ {3} \right] - \kappa a \left[ 1 - \frac {1}{2} (\kappa a) ^ {2} \right] \right\} \\ = - \frac {2 m V _ {0}}{\kappa^ {3} \hbar^ {2}} \frac {1}{3} (\kappa a) ^ {3} \\ = - \frac {2 m V _ {0} a ^ {3}}{3 \hbar^ {2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

这与方程 (10.82) 结果一样.

习题 10.11 计算方程 (10.91) 中的积分，并验证其右边的表达式

解答将 $\sin ( k r ) = { \frac { \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k r } - \mathrm { e } ^ { - \mathrm { i } k r } } { 2 \mathrm { i } } }$ ~ikr 代入方程 ，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} - \frac {2 m \beta}{\hbar^ {2} \kappa} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mu r} \left(\frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \kappa r} - \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \kappa r}}{2 \mathrm {i}}\right) \mathrm {d} r = - \frac {2 m \beta}{\hbar^ {2} \kappa} \frac {1}{2 \mathrm {i}} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {(- \mu + \mathrm {i} \kappa) r} - \mathrm {e} ^ {(- \mu - \mathrm {i} \kappa) r} \right] \mathrm {d} r \\ = - \frac {2 m \beta}{\hbar^ {2} \kappa} \frac {1}{2 \mathrm {i}} \left[ \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {(- \mu + \mathrm {i} \kappa) r}}{- \mu + \mathrm {i} \kappa} - \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {(- \mu - \mathrm {i} \kappa) r}}{- \mu - \mathrm {i} \kappa} \right] \Bigg | _ {0} ^ {\infty} \\ = - \frac {2 m \beta}{\hbar^ {2} \kappa} \frac {1}{2 \mathrm {i}} \left(\frac {1}{- \mu - \mathrm {i} \kappa} - \frac {1}{- \mu + \mathrm {i} \kappa}\right) \\ = - \frac {2 m \beta}{\hbar^ {2} (\mu^ {2} + \kappa^ {2})}. \\ \end{array}
$$

串串习题 10.12 在玻恩近似下，计算汤川势散射的总截面，用能量 $E$ 的函数来表示结果.

解答 习题 10.11 的结果即汤川势的散射振帽，汤川势的微分散射截面为散射掘幅的模平方，即

$$
D \left(\theta\right) = \left| f \left(\theta\right) \right| ^ {2} = \left(\frac {2 m \beta}{\hbar^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} \frac {1}{\left(\mu^ {2} + \kappa^ {2}\right) ^ {2}},
$$

总散射截面

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sigma = \int D (\theta) d \Omega \\ = \left(\frac {2 m \beta}{\hbar^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \int_ {0} ^ {2 \pi} \frac {1}{\left(\mu^ {2} + \kappa^ {2}\right) ^ {2}} \sin \theta \mathrm {d} \theta \mathrm {d} \phi \\ = \left(\frac {2 m \beta}{\hbar^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} 2 \pi \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \frac {1}{\left(\mu^ {2} + \kappa^ {2}\right) ^ {2}} \sin \theta d \theta . \\ \end{array}
$$

这里耍注意其中 $\kappa$ 是隐含角度。 $\theta$ 的，因为 $\kappa = 2 k \sin \left( \theta / 2 \right)$ 忽略掉常数系数，关键是要做下面这个积分:

$$
\int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \frac {1}{\left[ \mu^ {2} + 4 k ^ {2} \sin^ {2} (\theta / 2) \right] ^ {2}} \sin \theta \mathrm {d} \theta .
$$

利用 $\sin \theta = 2 \sin \left( \theta / 2 \right) \cos \left( \theta / 2 \right)$ ，并作变量代换 $t = 4 k ^ { 2 } \sin { ( \theta / 2 ) ^ { 2 } }$

$$
\mathrm {d} t = 4 k ^ {2} \sin (\theta / 2) \cos (\theta / 2) \mathrm {d} \theta = 2 k ^ {2} \sin \theta \mathrm {d} \theta , \quad t \in [ 0, 4 k ^ {2} ],
$$

得

$$
\frac {1}{2 k ^ {2}} \int_ {0} ^ {4 k ^ {2}} \frac {1}{(\mu^ {2} + t) ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} t = \frac {1}{2 k ^ {2}} \left(- \frac {1}{\mu^ {2} + t}\right) \bigg | _ {0} ^ {4 k ^ {2}} = \frac {2}{\mu^ {2} (\mu^ {2} + 4 k ^ {2})}.
$$

所以

$$
\sigma = 2 \pi \left(\frac {2 m \beta}{\hbar^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} \frac {2}{\mu^ {2} (\mu^ {2} + 4 k ^ {2})} = \pi \left(\frac {4 m \beta}{\mu \hbar^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} \frac {1}{\mu^ {2} + 4 k ^ {2}}.
$$

将k²= $k ^ { 2 } = { \frac { 2 m E } { \hbar ^ { 2 } } }$ 入上式，得

$$
\sigma = \pi \left(\frac {4 m \beta}{\mu \hbar}\right) ^ {2} \frac {1}{\hbar^ {2} \mu^ {2} + 8 m E}.
$$

*习题 10.13 对于习题 10.4中的势，

(a) 在低能玻恩近似下，计算 ${ \boldsymbol { f } } \left( { \boldsymbol { \theta } } \right) , D \left( { \boldsymbol { \theta } } \right)$ 和 $\sigma$   
(b) 在玻恩近似下，计算任意能量情况下的 $f \left( \theta \right)$ ;  
(c) 在适当的范围内，证明你的结果与习题 10.4的结果一致.

解答 (a) 习题 10.4中的势是 $\delta$ 函数球壳势，

$$
V (r) = \alpha \delta (r - a).
$$

利用低能玻恩近似公式很容易得到 $f \left( \theta \right) , D \left( \theta \right)$ ，即

$$
\begin{array}{l} f \left(\theta\right) = - \frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} V \left(r\right) 4 \pi r ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r \\ = - \frac {2 m}{\hbar^ {2}} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \alpha \delta (r - a) r ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r \\ = - \frac {2 m \alpha a ^ {2}}{\hbar^ {2}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
D (\theta) = | f (\theta) | ^ {2} = \left(\frac {2 m \alpha a ^ {2}}{\hbar^ {2}}\right) ^ {2}.
$$

由于 $D \left( \theta \right)$ 与角度无关，所以总散射截面就可以写成很筒单的形式，即

$$
\sigma = 4 \pi D (\theta) = \pi \left(\frac {4 m \alpha a ^ {2}}{\hbar^ {2}}\right).
$$

由于势是球对称的，直接套用球对称情形下的玻恩近似公式，得

$$
f (\theta) \cong - \frac {2 m}{\hbar^ {2} \kappa} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \alpha \delta (r - a) r \sin (\kappa r) \mathrm {d} r = - \frac {2 m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2} \kappa} a \sin (\kappa a),
$$

其中 $\kappa \equiv 2 k \sin \left( \theta / 2 \right)$ .

(c) 注意到习题 10.4中考虑的是低能情况， $k a \ll 1$ ，而 $\kappa = 2 k \sin \left( { \theta } / { 2 } \right)$ ，所 $\kappa a \ll 1$ 也成立在这种低能情况下， 中的 $f \left( \theta \right) \approx - \frac { 2 m \alpha a ^ { 2 } } { \hbar ^ { 2 } }$ 2mα α2引入 ， $\beta = \frac { 2 m \alpha a } { \hbar ^ { 2 } }$ ， 则 $f \left( \theta \right) \approx - a \beta$ 而习题 10.4中得到的1(9) 一 $f \left( \theta \right) = - \frac { a \beta } { 1 + \beta }$ 如果 $\beta \ll 1$ 两题的结果是一致的因为 $\beta = f / a \ll 1$ 时被捕相对于散射区域的比例，所以 $\beta \ll 1$ 是一个合理的条件.

习题 10.14 在冲量近似下，计算卢瑟福散射角。 $\theta$ (作为碰撞参数的函数).井证明:在适当的极限情况下所得结果与精确表达式(习题1O.1(a)) 一致.

![](images/3ac54e94daf846e3e3f85eb92e7d2d17cb27395f20955d40c5aac452cc05b65b.jpg)  
10.6

解答神量近似假设了粒子在整个散射过程始终沿着入射时的那条直线以恒定的初始速度运动然后考虑在该情况下垂直于运动方向的动量转移作为实际情况的近似.如图 10.6 所示.

$$
F _ {\perp} = \frac {1}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{r ^ {2}} \cos \phi , \quad \cos \phi = \frac {b}{r}, \quad r = \sqrt {x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}}.
$$

垂直方向上的神量为

$$
I _ {\perp} = \int F _ {\perp} \mathrm {d} t = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {F _ {\perp}}{v} \mathrm {d} x = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} v} \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{r ^ {2}} \frac {b}{r} \mathrm {d} x = \frac {b q _ {1} q _ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} v} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{(x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \mathrm {d} x,
$$

利用变量代换 $x = b \tan \alpha , \mathrm { d } x = \left( b / \cos ^ { 2 } \alpha \right) \mathrm { d } \alpha .$ ，上式的积分部分为

$$
\int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{(x ^ {2} + b ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \mathrm {d} x = \int_ {- \frac {\pi}{2}} ^ {\frac {\pi}{2}} \frac {\cos \alpha}{b ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} \alpha = \frac {2}{b ^ {2}},
$$

所以，

$$
I _ {\perp} = \frac {1}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {2 q _ {1} q _ {2}}{b v}.
$$

散射角为

$$
\theta \cong \arctan (I / p) = \arctan \left(\frac {1}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {2 q _ {1} q _ {2}}{b m v ^ {2}}\right) = \arctan \left(\frac {1}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{b E}\right).
$$

因此，

$$
b = \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} E \tan \theta}.
$$

假设散射角很小，则

$$
\tan \theta \approx \theta = 2 \frac {\theta}{2} \approx 2 \tan \left(\frac {\theta}{2}\right),
$$

因此 又可以写成

$$
b = \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} E \tan (\theta / 2)} = \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} E} \cot \left(\frac {\theta}{2}\right).
$$

这与习题 10.1(a) 中的精确表达式是一致的.

串串串习题 10.15 在二阶玻恩近似下，计算低能软球散射的散射振幅.

解答根据二阶近似公式(方程 (10.101))

$$
\begin{array}{l} \psi = \psi_ {0} (\boldsymbol {r}) + \int g (\boldsymbol {r} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \psi_ {0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) + \iint g (\boldsymbol {r} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \\ \cdot \left[ \int g \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {0} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {1}\right) V \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}\right) \psi_ {0} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}\right) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} \right] \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}, \\ \end{array}
$$

代入入射波(方程 (ο10.74)川)和格林函数(方程 (ο10.98))

$$
\psi_ {0} (\boldsymbol {r}) = A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k z},
$$

$$
g \left(\boldsymbol {r}\right) \equiv - \frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}}{r},
$$

得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \psi (\boldsymbol {r}) = A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k z} - \frac {m A}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}} \int \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k | \boldsymbol {r} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} |}}{| \boldsymbol {r} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} |} V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k z _ {0}} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} \\ + A \left(\frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} \int \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k | \boldsymbol {r} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} |}}{| \boldsymbol {r} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} |} V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \left[ \int \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k | \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} |}}{| \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} |} V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k z _ {1}} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} \right] \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}. \\ \end{array}
$$

在散射区域 $r \gg r _ { 0 }$ ，所以近似有

$$
k \left| \boldsymbol {r} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} \right| \approx k r - \boldsymbol {k} \cdot \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}, \quad \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k \left| \boldsymbol {r} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} \right|}}{\left| \boldsymbol {r} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} \right|} \approx \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}}{r} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \boldsymbol {k} \cdot \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}},
$$

其中 $\boldsymbol { k } = k \hat { \boldsymbol { r } }$ 在该近似下，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \psi (\boldsymbol {r}) = A \left\{\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k z} - \frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}}{r} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \boldsymbol {k} \cdot \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}} V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k z _ {0}} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} \right. \\ + \left(\frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k r}}{r} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \pmb {k} \cdot \pmb {r} _ {0}} V (\pmb {r} _ {0}) \left[ \int \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k | \pmb {r} _ {1} - \pmb {r} _ {0} |}}{| \pmb {r} _ {1} - \pmb {r} _ {0} |} V (\pmb {r} _ {1}) \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k z _ {1}} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \pmb {r} _ {1} \right] \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \pmb {r} _ {0} \Biggr \}. \\ \end{array}
$$

散射振幅是 _110 $\frac { \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k r } } { r }$ 前的系数，即

$$
\begin{array}{l} f (\theta , \phi) = - \frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {- i \boldsymbol {k} \cdot \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}} V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k z _ {0}} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} + \left(\frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \boldsymbol {k} \cdot \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}} V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \\ \cdot \left[ \int \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k | \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} |}}{| \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} |} V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k z _ {1}} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} \right] \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}. \\ \end{array}
$$

接下来我们还要考虑另一个近似，也就是低能散射近似.根据这个近似，指数部分都可认为近似为所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} f (\theta , \phi) \approx - \frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}} \int V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} + \left(\frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} \int V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}) \\ \cdot \left[ \int \frac {1}{| \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} |} V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} \right] \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {0}. \\ \end{array}
$$

对软球势

$$
V (r) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} V _ {0}, & r \leqslant a, \\ 0, & r > a. \end{array} \right.
$$

先处理

$$
\int \frac {1}{| \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} |} V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} = V _ {0} \int_ {0} ^ {a} \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \int_ {0} ^ {2 \pi} \frac {1}{| \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} |} r _ {1} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {1} \sin \theta_ {1} \mathrm {d} \theta_ {1} \mathrm {d} \phi_ {1},
$$

设 $z _ { 1 }$ 沿 $\mathbf { \delta } _ { \mathbf { r } _ { 0 } }$ 方向，所以 $\left| \pmb { r } _ { 0 } - \pmb { r } _ { 1 } \right| = \sqrt { r _ { 0 } ^ { 2 } + r _ { 1 } ^ { 2 } - 2 r _ { 0 } r _ { 1 } \cos \theta _ { 1 } }$ ，因此

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int \frac {1}{\sqrt {r _ {0} ^ {2} + r _ {1} ^ {2} - 2 r _ {0} r _ {1} \cos \theta}} V (r _ {1}) r _ {1} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {1} \mathrm {d} \Omega_ {1} \\ = 2 \pi V _ {0} \int_ {0} ^ {a} r _ {1} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {1} \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \frac {1}{\sqrt {r _ {0} ^ {2} + r _ {1} ^ {2} - 2 r _ {0} r _ {1} \cos \theta_ {1}}} \sin \theta_ {1} \mathrm {d} \theta_ {1} \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = 2 \pi V _ {0} \int_ {0} ^ {a} r _ {1} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {1} \left(\frac {1}{r _ {0} r _ {1}} \sqrt {r _ {0} ^ {2} + r _ {1} ^ {2} - 2 r _ {0} r _ {1} \cos \theta_ {1}} \Bigg | _ {0} ^ {\pi}\right) \\ = \frac {2 \pi V _ {0}}{r _ {0}} \int_ {0} ^ {a} r _ {1} \left[ \left(r _ {0} + r _ {1}\right) - \left| r _ {0} - r _ {1} \right| \right] d r _ {1}. \\ \end{array}
$$

当 $r _ { 1 } < r _ { 0 }$ 时， $\left( r _ { 0 } + r _ { 1 } \right) - \left| r _ { 0 } - r _ { 1 } \right| = 2 r _ { 1 }$ j $r _ { 1 } > r _ { 0 }$ 时， $\left( r _ { 0 } + r _ { 1 } \right) - \left| r _ { 0 } - r _ { 1 } \right| = 2 r _ { 0 }$

由于 $r _ { 0 } \ll a$ ，所以上式

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int \frac {1}{| \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} |} V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} = \frac {2 \pi V _ {0}}{r _ {0}} \left(\int_ {0} ^ {r _ {0}} 2 r _ {1} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {1} + r _ {0} \int_ {r _ {0}} ^ {a} 2 r _ {1} \mathrm {d} r _ {1}\right) \\ = 2 \pi V _ {0} \left(- \frac {1}{3} r _ {0} ^ {2} + a ^ {2}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

从而

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int V \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {0}\right) \left(\int \frac {1}{| \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} |} V \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}\right) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1}\right) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {0} \\ = 8 \pi^ {2} V _ {0} ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {a} \left(- \frac {1}{3} r _ {0} ^ {2} + a ^ {2}\right) r _ {0} ^ {2} d r _ {0} \\ = 8 \pi^ {2} V _ {0} ^ {2} \left(- \frac {1}{1 5} a ^ {5} + \frac {1}{3} a ^ {5}\right) \\ = \frac {3 2}{1 5} \pi^ {2} V _ {0} ^ {2} a ^ {5}. \\ \end{array}
$$

最后得到

$$
\begin{array}{l} f (\theta) = - \frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}} \frac {4}{3} \pi a ^ {3} V _ {0} + \left(\frac {m}{2 \pi \hbar^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} \frac {3 2}{1 5} \pi^ {2} V _ {0} ^ {2} a ^ {5} \\ = - \frac {2 m a ^ {3} V _ {0}}{3 \hbar^ {2}} \left(1 - \frac {4}{5} \frac {V _ {0} m a ^ {2}}{\hbar^ {2}}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

# 补充苟题

串串*习题 10.16 求解一维薛定海方程的格林函数，并利用它构造其积分形式(类似于方程 (10.66)).

解答类比方程 (10.52) (10.54)，在一维情况下，格林函数满足

$$
\left(\frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} + k ^ {2}\right) G (x) = \delta (x),
$$

荆 $G \left( x \right) = \frac { 1 } { \sqrt { 2 \pi } } \int \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } s x } g \left( s \right) \mathrm { d } s$ 所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(\frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} + k ^ {2}\right) G (x) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi}} \int (- s ^ {2} + k ^ {2}) \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} s x} g (s) \mathrm {d} s = \delta (x) = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} s x} \mathrm {d} s \\ \Rightarrow g (s) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi} (- s ^ {2} + k ^ {2})}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
G (x) = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \int \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} s x}}{k ^ {2} - s ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} s = - \frac {1}{2 \pi} \int \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} s x}}{(s + k) (s - k)} \mathrm {d} s.
$$

将 $s$ 扩展到复平面，该积分有两个极点 $s = \pm k$ ，对 $x > 0$ ，积分围道应在上半平面，利用留数定理

$$
G (x) = - \frac {1}{2 \pi} \oint \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} s x}}{(s + k) (s - k)} \mathrm {d} s = 2 \pi \mathrm {i} \left. \left[ - \frac {1}{2 \pi} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} s x}}{s + k} \right] \right| _ {s = k} = - \mathrm {i} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x}}{2 k}.
$$

对 $x < 0$ 积分围道应在下半平面，

$$
G (x) = + \frac {1}{2 \pi} \oint \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} s x}}{(s + k) (s - k)} \mathrm {d} s = 2 \pi \mathrm {i} \left[ \frac {1}{2 \pi} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} s x}}{s - k} \right] \Bigg | _ {s = - k} = - \mathrm {i} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x}}{2 k},
$$

所以在两种情况下，都可以把 $G \left( x \right)$ 表示为

$$
G \left(x\right) = - \mathrm {i} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k | x |}}{2 k}.
$$

所以由方程 (10.67) ，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \psi (x) = \psi_ {0} (x) + \frac {2 m}{\hbar^ {2}} \int G (x - x _ {0}) V (x _ {0}) \psi (x _ {0}) d x _ {0} \\ = \psi_ {0} (x) - \mathrm {i} \frac {m}{k \hbar^ {2}} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k | x - x _ {0} |} V (x _ {0}) \psi (x _ {0}) \mathrm {d} x _ {0}, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $\psi _ { 0 } \left( x \right)$ 满足齐师胡耀 $\left( { \frac { \mathrm { d } ^ { 2 } } { \mathrm { d } x ^ { 2 } } } + k ^ { 2 } \right) \psi _ { 0 } \left( x \right) = 0 .$

事*习题 10.17 利用习题 10.16 的结果推导一维散射的玻恩近似(在区间 $- \infty <$ $x < \infty$ 内，原点处无"砖墙").也就是，选择 $\psi _ { 0 } \left( x \right) = A \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k x }$ ，且假定 $\psi \left( x _ { 0 } \right) \approx \psi _ { 0 } \left( x _ { 0 } \right)$ 来计算积分.证明:反射系数具有如下形式

$$
R \approx \left(\frac {m}{\hbar^ {2} k}\right) ^ {2} \left| \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {2 \mathrm {i} k x} V (x) \mathrm {d} x \right| ^ {2}.
$$

解答由玻恩近似，令 $\psi _ { 0 } \left( x \right) = A \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k x _ { 0 } } , \psi \left( x \right) \approx A \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k x }$ ，则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \psi (x) = A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} - \frac {\mathrm {i} m}{\hbar^ {2} k} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {x} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k | x - x _ {0} |} V (x _ {0}) A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x _ {0}} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \\ = A \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} - \frac {\mathrm {i} m}{\hbar^ {2} k} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {x} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k (x - x _ {0})} V (x _ {0}) \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x _ {0}} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} - \frac {\mathrm {i} m}{\hbar^ {2} k} \int_ {x} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k (x _ {0} - x)} V (x _ {0}) \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x _ {0}} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right] \\ = A \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} - \frac {\mathrm {i} m}{\hbar^ {2} k} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {x} V (x _ {0}) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} - \frac {\mathrm {i} m}{\hbar^ {2} k} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x} \int_ {x} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {2 \mathrm {i} k x _ {0}} V (x _ {0}) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right] \\ = A \left[ 1 - \frac {\mathrm {i} m}{\hbar^ {2} k} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {x} V (x _ {0}) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} - A \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i} m}{\hbar^ {2} k} \int_ {x} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {2 \mathrm {i} k x _ {0}} V (x _ {0}) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x}. \\ \end{array}
$$

由于势是局域的，那么当 $x \to \infty$ 时，上面的最后一项为零，

$$
\psi \left(x\right) = A \left[ 1 - \frac {\mathrm {i} m}{\hbar^ {2} k} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} V \left(x _ {0}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x},
$$

显然这是透射波.当 $x \to - \infty$ 时，第一个积分为零，

$$
\psi (x) = A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} - A \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i} m}{\hbar^ {2} k} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {2 \mathrm {i} k x _ {0}} V (x _ {0}) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x},
$$

显然 第一项代表入射波，第二项代表反射披，所以反射系数是

$$
R \approx \left(\frac {m}{\hbar^ {2} k}\right) ^ {2} \left| \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {2 \mathrm {i} k x} V (x) \mathrm {d} x \right| ^ {2}.
$$

如果我们试图计算透射系数

$$
T = \left| 1 - \frac {\mathrm {i} m}{\hbar^ {2} k} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} V (x _ {0}) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right| ^ {2} = 1 + \left[ \frac {m}{\hbar^ {2} k} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} V (x _ {0}) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right] ^ {2}.
$$

这显然不合理'因为 $T > 1$ ，所以玻恩一阶近似可以给出反射系数，但是不能给出合理的透射系数，应当使用 $T = 1 - R$ 来计算透射系数.

习题 10.18 利用一维玻恩近似(习题 10.17)，分别计算 $\delta$ 函数(方程 (2.117)) 和有限方势阱(方程 (2.148)) 散射的透射系数 $( T = 1 - R )$ 并将所得结果与精确解(方(2.144) (2.172)) 作对比

解答对 $\delta$ 函数势 $V \left( x \right) = - \alpha \delta \left( x \right)$ 由上题结果知

$$
R = \left(\frac {m}{\hbar^ {2} k}\right) ^ {2} \left| - \alpha \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {2 \mathrm {i} k x} \delta (x) \mathrm {d} x \right| ^ {2} = \left(\frac {m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2} k}\right) ^ {2}.
$$

用能量 $E = \hbar ^ { 2 } k ^ { 2 } / 2 m$ 重新表示结果为

$$
R = \frac {m \alpha^ {2}}{2 \hbar^ {2} E}, \quad T = 1 - \frac {m \alpha^ {2}}{2 \hbar^ {2} E}.
$$

而精确结果(方程 (2.144)) $T = 1 \bigg / \bigg ( 1 + \frac { m \alpha ^ { 2 } } { 2 \hbar ^ { 2 } E } \bigg ) \approx 1 - \frac { m \alpha ^ { 2 } } { 2 \hbar ^ { 2 } E }$ ma² ma² 当 ma² $\frac { m \alpha ^ { 2 } } { 2 \hbar ^ { 2 } E } \ll 1$ 时，两者是一致的.'对有限方势阱

$$
V \left(x\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} {- V _ {0},} & {- a <   x <   a,} \\ {0,} & {\text {其 他}.} \end{array} \right.
$$

则

$$
R = \left(\frac {m}{\hbar^ {2} k}\right) ^ {2} \left| - V _ {0} \int_ {- a} ^ {a} \mathrm {e} ^ {2 \mathrm {i} k x} \mathrm {d} x \right| ^ {2} = \left(\frac {m}{\hbar^ {2} k}\right) ^ {2} \left[ V _ {0} \frac {\sin (2 k a)}{k} \right] ^ {2},
$$

透射系数为

$$
T = 1 - \left[ \frac {V _ {0}}{2 E} \sin \left(\frac {2 a}{\hbar} \sqrt {2 m E}\right) \right] ^ {2},
$$

当 $E \gg V _ { 0 }$ 时，精确解(方程 (2.172))

$$
T ^ {- 1} \approx 1 + \left[ \frac {V _ {0}}{2 E} \sin \left(\frac {2 a}{\hbar} \sqrt {2 m E}\right) \right] ^ {2},
$$

$$
T = 1 - \left[ \frac {V _ {0}}{2 E} \sin \left(\frac {2 a}{\hbar} \sqrt {2 m E}\right) \right] ^ {2},
$$

所以两者结果是一致的.

习题 10.19 证明:光学定理，它将总截面和向前散射振幅的虚部联系起来，

$$
\sigma = \frac {4 \pi}{k} \mathrm {I m} [ f (0) ].
$$

提示利用方程 (10.47) (10 .48).

证明由方程 (10.47)

$$
f \left(\theta\right) = \frac {1}{k} \sum_ {\ell = 0} ^ {\infty} \left(2 \ell + 1\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \delta_ {\ell}} \sin \left(\delta_ {\ell}\right) P _ {\ell} (\cos \theta),
$$

由于 $P _ { \ell } \left( 1 \right) = 1$ ，所以

$$
f (0) = \frac {1}{k} \sum_ {\ell = 0} ^ {\infty} (2 \ell + 1) e ^ {i \delta_ {\ell}} \sin (\delta_ {\ell}).
$$

取 $f \left( 0 \right)$ 的虚部

$$
\operatorname {I m} [ f (0) ] = \frac {1}{k} \sum_ {\ell = 0} ^ {\infty} (2 \ell + 1) \sin^ {2} (\delta_ {\ell}),
$$

由总散射截面(方程 (10 .48))

$$
\sigma = \frac {4 \pi}{k ^ {2}} \sum_ {\ell = 0} ^ {\infty} (2 \ell + 1) \sin^ {2} (\delta_ {\ell}),
$$

所以

$$
\sigma = \frac {4 \pi}{k} \operatorname {I m} [ f (0) ].
$$

习题 10.20 使用玻恩近似确定高斯势散射的总截面，

$$
V (\boldsymbol {r}) = A \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mu r ^ {2}}.
$$

用常数 $\mu$ m(入射粒子质量) $k \equiv { \sqrt { 2 m E } } { \Big / } \hbar$ 表示你的结果，其中 $E$ 是功能量.

解答由方程 (10.88) ，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} f (\theta) = - \frac {2 m}{\hbar^ {2} \kappa} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} r A \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mu r ^ {2}} \sin (\kappa r) \mathrm {d} r \\ = - \frac {2 m A}{\hbar^ {2} \kappa} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \sin (\kappa r) \mathrm {d} \left(- \frac {1}{2 \mu} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mu r ^ {2}}\right) \\ = \frac {2 m A}{2 \mu \hbar^ {2} \kappa} \left\{\mathrm {e} ^ {- \mu r ^ {2}} \sin (\kappa r) \Big | _ {0} ^ {\infty} - \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mu r ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} [ \sin (\kappa r) ] \right\} \\ = \frac {m A}{\mu \hbar^ {2} \kappa} \left[ 0 - k \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mu r ^ {2}} \cos (\kappa r) \mathrm {d} r \right] \\ = - \frac {m A}{\mu \hbar^ {2}} \left(\frac {\sqrt {\pi}}{2 \sqrt {\mu}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \kappa^ {2} / 4 \mu}\right) \\ = - \frac {m A \sqrt {\pi}}{2 \mu^ {3 / 2} \hbar^ {2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \kappa^ {2} / 4 \mu}, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $\kappa = 2 k \sin \left( \theta / 2 \right)$

由方程 (10.14)

$$
D (\theta) = \frac {\mathrm {d} \sigma}{\mathrm {d} \Omega} = | f (\theta) | ^ {2} = \frac {\pi m ^ {2} A ^ {2}}{4 \mu^ {3} \hbar^ {4}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \kappa^ {2} / (2 \mu)},
$$

困此，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sigma = \int \frac {\mathrm {d} \sigma}{\mathrm {d} \Omega} \mathrm {d} \Omega = \frac {\pi m ^ {2} A ^ {2}}{4 \mu^ {3} \hbar^ {4}} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {- 4 k ^ {2} \sin^ {2} (\theta / 2) / (2 \mu)} \sin \theta \mathrm {d} \theta \mathrm {d} \phi \\ = \frac {\pi^ {2} m ^ {2} A ^ {2}}{2 \mu^ {3} \hbar^ {4}} \int \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 k ^ {2} \sin^ {2} (\theta / 2) / \mu} \sin \theta \mathrm {d} \theta \mathrm {d} \phi . \\ \end{array}
$$

己知 $\sin \theta = 2 \sin \left( \theta / 2 \right) \cos \left( \theta / 2 \right)$ ，令 $z = \sin \left( \theta / 2 \right)$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sigma = \frac {\pi^ {2} m ^ {2} A ^ {2}}{2 \mu^ {3} \hbar^ {4}} \int_ {0} ^ {1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 k ^ {2} x ^ {2} / \mu} 4 x \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {2 \pi^ {2} m ^ {2} A ^ {2}}{\mu^ {3} \hbar^ {4}} \int_ {0} ^ {1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 k ^ {2} x ^ {2} / \mu} x \mathrm {d} x \\ = \left. \frac {2 \pi^ {2} m ^ {2} A ^ {2}}{\mu^ {3} \hbar^ {4}} \left(- \frac {\mu}{4 k ^ {2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 k ^ {2} x ^ {2} / \mu}\right) \right| _ {0} ^ {1} \\ = \frac {\pi^ {2} m ^ {2} A ^ {2}}{2 \mu^ {2} \hbar^ {4} k ^ {2}} \left(1 - \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 k ^ {2} / \mu}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$
